### 📷 例 651：棕色系时尚编辑风情侣拼贴画

![棕色系时尚编辑风情侣拼贴画](../images/%E6%A3%95%E8%89%B2%E7%B3%BB%E6%97%B6%E5%B0%9A%E7%BC%96%E8%BE%91%E9%A3%8E%E6%83%85%E4%BE%A3%E6%8B%BC%E8%B4%B4%E7%94%BB.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：创作一张超写实的时尚编辑风格拼贴画，展示一对时尚的年轻情侣，背景为温暖的单色棕色影棚，采用电影感剪贴簿布局，由撕纸风格的照片碎片构成。

画布：竖向肖像图，2:3 纵横比，高分辨率时尚编辑质感。所有碎片均采用带有纹理的斑驳棕色影棚背景，配合温暖的柔光箱照明、柔和的阴影以及统一的深褐色调。

布局：排列 5 张由不规则乳白色撕裂边缘分隔的撕纸风格照片碎片。碎片 1 为左侧的大型全身竖向照片，展示情侣站在一起；碎片 2 为右上方的宽幅中景坐姿照；碎片 3 为中右侧的横向裁剪图，展示两人坐着时亲密靠在一起；碎片 4 为左下方的坐姿全身/四分之三身照；碎片 5 为右下方的紧凑特写肖像，展示两人的面部和肩部。撕裂边缘应呈现不均匀、手工制作且层叠的剪贴簿效果，画面中不含任何文字或标志。

主体：每张碎片中均出现这对情侣。女性为 {argument name="woman description" default="一位留着深色长波浪卷发的时尚年轻女性"}；男性为 {argument name="man description" default="一位留着深色短波浪卷发的时尚年轻男性"}。他们应以亲密的时尚情侣姿态自然摆拍，神情放松且自信，有时互相倚靠或坐在一起。保持真实的人体比例和编辑风格的姿势。

服装：温暖的棕色系单色造型。女性穿着 {argument name="woman outfit" default="深棕色皮质西装外套内搭奶油色心形领上衣、缎面棕色迷你裙、深色透肤连裤袜及棕色尖头高跟鞋"}。男性穿着 {argument name="man outfit" default="棕褐色麂皮衬衫式外套内搭白色背心、深棕色长裤、黑色乐福鞋及银色腕表"}。确保 5 张碎片中的服装保持一致。

视觉风格：超写实 DSLR 影棚摄影，柔和的暖色高光，高端时尚杂志质感，特写镜头具有浅景深效果，丰富的棕色纹理，精致而自然的皮肤质感，电影般的编辑氛围。构图简洁，细节真实，包括麂皮质感、皮革光泽、缎面裙光泽、透肤丝袜及金属腕表反光。

约束条件：仅限 2 人，且必须为 5 张照片碎片。无标题，无水印，无额外道具，无额外人物。面部可进行柔和的匿名处理或保持模糊，但整体画面必须呈现出高端情侣摄影拼贴画的效果。

[English]
Goal: Create an ultra-realistic editorial fashion collage of a stylish young couple in a warm monochromatic brown studio setting, with a cinematic scrapbook layout made from torn-paper photo fragments.

Canvas: Vertical portrait image, 2:3 aspect ratio, high-resolution fashion editorial look. Use a textured mottled brown studio backdrop across all fragments, with warm softbox lighting, gentle shadows, and a cohesive sepia-brown color grade.

Layout: Arrange exactly 5 torn-paper photo fragments separated by thick irregular cream-white ripped edges. Fragment 1 is a large full-body vertical photo on the left showing the couple standing together. Fragment 2 is a wide upper-right seated close medium shot of both people. Fragment 3 is a middle-right horizontal crop showing the couple leaning close while seated. Fragment 4 is a bottom-left seated full/three-quarter shot of both people. Fragment 5 is a bottom-right tight close-up portrait crop of both faces and shoulders. Make the torn seams uneven, handmade, and layered like a scrapbook collage, with no text or logos.

Subjects: The couple appears in every fragment. The woman is {argument name="woman description" default="a stylish young woman with long dark wavy hair"}; the man is {argument name="man description" default="a stylish young man with short dark wavy hair"}. They should pose naturally as an intimate fashion couple, looking relaxed and confident, sometimes leaning on each other or sitting closely together. Preserve realistic body proportions and editorial posing.

Wardrobe: Warm brown monochromatic styling. The woman wears {argument name="woman outfit" default="a dark brown leather blazer over a cream sweetheart-neck top, a satin brown mini skirt, sheer dark tights, and pointed brown heels"}. The man wears {argument name="man outfit" default="a tan suede overshirt jacket over a white tank top, dark brown trousers, black loafers, and a silver wristwatch"}. Keep the clothing consistent across all 5 fragments.

Visual style: Ultra-realistic DSLR studio photography, soft warm highlights, premium fashion magazine styling, shallow depth of field in the close-ups, rich brown textures, polished but natural skin, cinematic editorial mood. Use clean composition and realistic fabric details: suede texture, leather sheen, satin skirt shine, sheer tights, and metallic watch reflections.

Constraints: Exactly 2 people and exactly 5 photo fragments. No captions, no watermark, no extra props, no extra people. Faces may be softly anonymized or left non-specific, but the overall image must read as a high-end couple photoshoot collage.
```

### 📷 例 652：东亚女性生活方式拼贴画

一个 3×2 的竖版编辑肖像拼贴画，包含六张生活方式抓拍照片，主角是一位美丽的年轻 {argument name="ethnicity" default="东亚"} 女性，留着一头乌黑亮丽的长发，扎成随性的高马尾，配有轻盈的空气刘海，皮肤白皙透亮，五官精致柔美，眼神灵动，气质自然优雅。拍摄场景位于一个 {argument name="setting" default="温馨现代卧室"} 内，环境光温暖，铺着米色床品，挂着柔软的窗帘，墙上装饰着相框，床头灯散发着柔光，呈现出宁静的韩式极简室内美学。服装：配套的奢华家居服套装，色调为温暖的 {argument name="outfit color" default="燕麦米色"}，包括柔软的罗纹短款吊带背心、宽松的高腰休闲裤，以及一件随意披在肩上的超大廓形纹理针织开衫。高级针织面料，纹理清晰，褶皱自然。每一帧都捕捉了不同的放松姿态和表情：1. 盘腿坐在床上，双肩微前倾，直视镜头，露出温柔的微笑。2. 舒适地坐着，一只袖子半遮住手，闭眼自然微笑，仿佛正在回味一件乐事。3. 身体微侧，下巴轻靠在手上，若有所思地看向画框外。4. 望向窗户，温暖的阳光照亮了她的侧脸，姿态放松，表情自然。5. 一手托腮，俏皮地眨眼并露出浅笑。6. 侧坐在床上，双膝微蜷，凝视远方，表情宁静沉思。光影：柔和的晨光与黄金时刻的窗光，温暖的实用台灯光，皮肤上的细腻高光，真实的阴影，温馨的氛围照明。摄影风格：生活方式抓拍，韩系时尚编辑风格，私密卧室摄影，真实表情，放松的肢体语言，自然的皮肤纹理，清晰的织物细节，浅景深，电影感暖色调，写实色彩分级。构图：六个等间距的竖版肖像面板，排列成整洁的 3×2 拼贴布局，每一帧都呈现独特的姿态与氛围，同时保持视觉一致性。氛围：温馨、私密、柔美、宁静、诱人、放松的奢华感、安静的周末早晨美学。超写实，照片级真实，高细节，高级编辑摄影，Vogue Korea 灵感，自然美，背景虚化，8K 画质。

[English]
A 3×2 vertical editorial portrait collage featuring six candid lifestyle photographs of a beautiful young {argument name="ethnicity" default="East Asian"} woman with long glossy black hair styled in a loose high ponytail, soft wispy bangs, fair luminous skin, delicate feminine features, expressive eyes, and a naturally elegant appearance. She is photographed inside a {argument name="setting" default="cozy modern bedroom"} with warm ambient lighting, cream-colored bedding, soft curtains, framed photos on the wall, a glowing bedside lamp, and a calm minimalist Korean interior aesthetic. Outfit: matching luxury loungewear set in warm {argument name="outfit color" default="oatmeal-beige"} tones, consisting of a soft ribbed cropped camisole, relaxed high-waisted lounge pants, and an oversized textured knit cardigan draped casually off one shoulder. ### 📷 例 677：土耳其烤肉 (Döner Kebab) 艺术美食摄影

![土耳其烤肉 (Döner Kebab) 艺术美食摄影](../images/%E5%9C%9F%E8%80%B3%E5%85%B6%E7%83%A4%E8%82%89%20(D%C3%B6ner%20Kebab)%20%E8%89%BA%E6%9C%AF%E7%BE%8E%E9%A3%9F%E6%91%84%E5%BD%B1.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
8K 超高清超写实商业美食摄影，3:4 画幅。共 6 个场景，每个场景均采用纯色或渐变背景：

场景 1，烤肉切片爆炸：{argument name="food" default="传统土耳其烤肉（牛羊肉混合）"}，薄如纸的肉片在半空中向外螺旋飞散，白色蒜酱和红色辣椒酱四溅，新鲜欧芹叶漂浮其中。{argument name="background" default="深绯红色背景"}。

场景 2，Dürüm 卷饼悬浮：高级 Dürüm 卷饼从中间切开并垂直悬浮，横截面展示出层叠的烤肉、生菜、番茄和洋葱，白色蒜味酸奶酱优雅地淋下，细微的香料颗粒飘散。温暖的赤陶橙色背景。

场景 3，酱汁倾倒瞬间：一堆新鲜切片的烤肉，边缘焦脆，浓郁的奶油蒜味酸奶酱从上方倾泻而下，定格在流动瞬间，辛辣的红辣椒酱伴随细长的绯红色流线淋下，下方点缀着番茄片和欧芹，热气升腾。深炭黑色背景。

场景 4，解构主义构图：烤过的 Lavash 面包片、烤肉切片、番茄片、生菜叶和洋葱圈分别悬浮在不同高度，光亮的酱汁线条将各元素艺术化地连接在一起，空气中弥漫着超细的香料粉末。柔和的鼠尾草绿色背景。

场景 5，旋转烤肉特写：垂直烤肉柱的极致特写，一把大烤肉刀定格在切肉瞬间，新鲜肉片掉落，焦脆的碎屑和香料颗粒在空中飞舞，新鲜切口处热气升腾。浓郁的琥珀金色背景。

场景 6，俯视盘中爆炸：俯视视角，所有食材以圆形图案向上迸发，烤肉切片、炸薯条、烤辣椒和番茄、新鲜欧芹、漆树粉、柠檬角，酱汁液滴喷溅，各元素处于不同高度并伴有旋转感。带有暗角的深勃艮第红背景。

全局设置：{argument name="lighting" default="受控摄影棚灯光"}，强调肉质纹理和焦痕，浅至中等景深，丰富的对比度，温暖的咸鲜色调，自然光泽，诱人的色彩分级。无文字、无 Logo、无人像、无手部、无卡通风格，无塑料感食物。

[English]
8K UHD hyper-realistic commercial food photography, 3:4 aspect ratio. 6 scenes, each on its own solid or gradient background:

Scene 1, Döner slice explosion: {argument name="food" default="Traditional Turkish döner (beef and lamb mix)"}, paper-thin ribbons spiraling outward mid-air, white garlic sauce and red chili sauce splashing, fresh parsley leaves floating. {argument name="background" default="Deep crimson red background"}.

Scene 2, Dürüm wrap floating: Premium dürüm cut in half and floating vertically, cross-section revealing döner meat, lettuce, tomatoes, onions layered inside, white garlic yogurt sauce drizzling elegantly, subtle spice particles drifting. Warm terracotta orange background.

Scene 3, Sauce pour drama: Mound of freshly sliced döner with crispy charred edges, thick creamy garlic yogurt sauce pouring from above frozen mid-flow, spicy red chili sauce drizzling alongside in thin crimson streams, sliced tomatoes and parsley below, heat vapor rising. Dark charcoal black background.

Scene 4, Deconstructed composition: Toasted lavash bread pieces, döner slices, tomato slices, lettuce leaves, and onion rings all suspended separately at varying heights, glossy sauce ribbons connecting elements artistically, ultra-fine spice dust in the air. Muted sage green background.

Scene 5, Rotating spit close-up: Extreme close-up of vertical döner tower on spit, large döner knife frozen mid-slice, fresh slice falling away, charred bits and seasoning particles in air, heat vapor rising from the fresh cut. Rich golden amber background.

Scene 6, Overhead plate explosion: Top-down view, all ingredients bursting upward in circular pattern, döner slices, french fries, grilled peppers and tomatoes, fresh parsley, sumac, lemon wedges, sauce droplets spraying, elements at varying heights with some rotating. Deep burgundy red background with vignette.

Global: {argument name="lighting" default="controlled studio lighting"} emphasizing meat texture and char marks, shallow to medium depth of field, rich contrast, warm savory tones, natural shine, appetizing color grading. No text, logos, people, hands, cartoon style, or plastic-looking food.
```

```text
[中文]
影棚人像，紧凑的头部与肩部特写，平视视角，近乎完美的中心对称。焦点清晰聚焦于面部，浅景深，纯黑色背景。主体：中年男性，正面姿势，表情冷静自信，眼神直视镜头。短小的灰白胡茬和修剪整齐的短山羊胡，可见毛孔和细纹，呈现逼真的皮肤细节，浅色眼睛带有眼神光。中等长度深棕色头发，带有细微的灰色挑染，向后梳理且略显凌乱。身穿白色圆领 T 恤和深色夹克或外套，隐约可见细项链。光影：电影级高对比度霓虹分色光影。左前方冷青色/蓝色主光作为主要照明。右后方饱和的红色/洋红色轮廓光，在头发和面部轮廓上形成强烈的边缘高光。补光极少。纯黑色背景，主体与背景分离度高。调色板：青蓝色、深红色、黑色。光影采用高饱和度霓虹色调，服装色调中性。赛博朋克风格的分色调色。技术参数：专业影棚摄影，85mm 人像镜头压缩感，高清晰度，低噪点，受控的高光与深邃的阴影，增强的微对比度。风格：电影级新黑色电影赛博朋克编辑人像。大胆、现代、高冲击力。关键词：电影级霓虹人像，蓝红分色光影，黑色背景，强烈的直视目光，赛博朋克调色，高对比度影棚布光。

[English]
Studio portrait, tight head-and-shoulders close-up, eye-level, near-perfect central symmetry. Sharp focus on face with shallow depth of field, pure black background. Subject: middle-aged male, frontal pose, calm and confident expression with intense direct gaze. Short salt-and-pepper stubble and trimmed goatee, visible pores and fine lines for realistic skin detail, light-colored eyes with catchlights. Medium-length dark brown hair with subtle gray highlights, brushed back and slightly tousled. Wearing a white crew-neck t-shirt and dark jacket or coat, thin chain necklace partially visible. Lighting: cinematic high-contrast neon split lighting. Cool cyan/blue key light from front-left as dominant illumination. Saturated red/magenta rim light from rear-right creating strong edge highlight on hair and face contour. Minimal fill light. Pure black background with strong subject-background separation. Color palette: cyan blue, deep red, black. High saturation neon tones on lighting, neutral clothing. Cyberpunk-inspired split-toning color grading. Technical: professional studio photography, 85mm portrait lens compression, high sharpness, minimal noise, controlled highlights with deep shadows, enhanced micro-contrast. Style: cinematic neo-noir cyberpunk editorial portrait. Bold, modern, high-impact. Keywords: cinematic neon portrait, blue and red split lighting, black background, intense direct gaze, cyberpunk color grading, high-contrast studio lighting.

### 📷 例 685：米色男装 Lookbook 拼贴画

![米色男装 Lookbook 拼贴画](../images/%E7%B1%B3%E8%89%B2%E7%94%B7%E8%A3%85%20Lookbook%20%E6%8B%BC%E8%B4%B4%E7%94%BB.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：创建一个方形男士时尚 Lookbook 拼贴画，展示 {argument name="model description" default="一位身材匀称、留着深色短发和修剪整齐胡须的成年男性"} 穿着单色米色街头服饰，每张画面中的脸部均通过柔和的矩形模糊或纯色肤色隐私遮挡块进行匿名化处理。

画布：1:1 方形图像，温暖的时尚编辑摄影风格，高分辨率，自然阳光，米色和沙色调色板。

布局：使用 5 个由干净白色间隙分隔的照片面板。左侧为 2x2 网格，包含四张较小的全身穿搭照。右侧为一张占据全高的垂直大尺寸特写肖像面板。

面板数量及内容：
1. 左上面板：全身行走姿势，身体略微前倾，双手放松，露出白色运动鞋，背景为仙人掌和沙漠植物。
2. 左中/左上网格面板：正面四分之三侧身姿势，模特视线微垂，双手在胸前交叠，可见连帽衫细节。
3. 左下面板：从左侧拍摄的侧面/背面行走姿势，可见连帽衫兜帽和工装裤轮廓。
4. 左中/左下网格面板：从右侧拍摄的侧面行走姿势，抬起一只脚，展示锥形工装裤和白色运动鞋。
5. 右侧大面板：从胸部到头部的近距离裁剪肖像，脸部中心覆盖有超大隐私矩形，突显米色连帽衫面料质感，可见金链项链，手部抬至胸前并佩戴戒指。

主体细节：穿搭由 4 件主要可见时尚单品组成：{argument name="hoodie color" default="沙米色"} 套头连帽衫、配套修身工装慢跑裤（带侧袋）、干净的白色低帮运动鞋以及金链项链。在一只手腕上巧妙地添加手表或手链，并在一个手指上佩戴戒指。保持极简、昂贵的休闲感、中性色调和协调感。

环境：极简沙漠风格室内摄影棚，带有纹理的棕褐色灰泥墙面，混凝土底板，温暖的阳光投射出柔和的阴影，狭窄的建筑开口，左侧面板附近放置稀疏的仙人掌或多肉植物。

视觉风格：照片级真实感的时尚编辑风格，温暖的黄金时刻光影，特写镜头中浅景深，清晰的服装质感，柔和的米色单色调，干净的 Instagram Lookbook 构图。

约束条件：无文字，无 Logo，无水印。严格保持五面板拼贴布局。所有面板中的脸部必须保持遮挡。除米色、白色、金色和自然沙漠色调外，不得添加额外人物或其它服装颜色。

[English]
Goal: Create a square men's fashion lookbook collage featuring {argument name="model description" default="a fit adult man with short dark hair and trimmed beard"} wearing a monochrome beige streetwear outfit, with the face anonymized by a soft rectangular blur or solid skin-tone privacy block in every view.

Canvas: 1:1 square image, warm editorial fashion photography, high resolution, natural sunlight, beige and sand color palette.

Layout: Use exactly 5 photo panels separated by clean white gutters. The left half is a 2-by-2 grid of four smaller full-body outfit shots. The right half is one large vertical close-up portrait panel spanning the full height.

Panel count and contents:
1. Top-left panel: full-body walking pose angled slightly forward, hands relaxed, white sneakers visible, cactus and desert plants in the background.
2. Top-middle/upper-left grid panel: front-facing three-quarter pose, model looking down slightly, hands clasped near chest, hoodie details visible.
3. Bottom-left panel: side/back walking pose from the left, hoodie hood and cargo pants silhouette visible.
4. Bottom-middle/lower-left grid panel: side walking pose from the right, one foot lifted, showing the tapered cargo pants and white sneakers.
5. Right large panel: close cropped upper-body portrait from chest to head, oversized face privacy rectangle centered over the face, beige hoodie fabric texture prominent, gold chain necklace visible, hand raised near chest with a ring.

Subject details: Outfit consists of exactly 4 main visible fashion pieces: {argument name="hoodie color" default="sand beige"} pullover hoodie, matching slim cargo jogger pants with side pockets, clean white low-top sneakers, and a gold chain necklace. Add a watch or bracelet subtly on one wrist and a ring on one finger. Keep the styling minimal, expensive casual, neutral-toned, and coordinated.

Environment: Minimal desert-inspired indoor studio with textured tan plaster walls, concrete floor, warm sunlight casting soft shadows, narrow architectural openings, and sparse cacti or succulent plants placed near the left panels.

Visual style: Photorealistic fashion editorial, warm golden-hour lighting, shallow depth of field in the close-up, crisp clothing texture, muted beige monochrome grading, clean Instagram lookbook composition.

Constraints: No text, no logos, no watermark. Preserve the five-panel collage exactly. The face must remain obscured in all panels. Do not add extra people or extra clothing colors beyond the beige, white, gold, and natural desert tones.
```

**来源：** [@Cherry 2.O](https://x.com/Mind_Boticni/status/2063079795219656927) | 2026-06-06

[中文]
[主体姿势与表情]

蹲在洁白的沙滩上，一只手拿着一个大大的 {argument name="drink" default="绿色生椰子"}，摆出仿佛正在用吸管喝水的姿势。直视镜头，双唇微嘟，双颊鼓起，表情可爱活泼，眼神充满神秘感。

[主体服装]

身穿一件 {argument name="outfit color" default="天蓝色"} 针织短款上衣，肩带较宽，可见细罗纹编织纹理，搭配由 {argument name="bottom material" default="亚麻"} 制成的白色短裤。整体造型增添了清爽明亮的夏日氛围。

[发型与妆容细节]

高盘发造型，自然的碎发在脸颊两侧呈曲线垂落。盘发侧面和腰间饰有巨大的淡粉色 3D 玫瑰装饰。妆容为“宿醉妆”，在脸颊和鼻梁处涂抹了大面积鲜艳的粉色腮红，并配有长而尖的裸色美甲。

[光影与方向]

海滩晴天强烈的自然光从人物后方照射，在脸部和身体前方投下自然的阴影。明亮的阳光生动地突显了肌肤的光泽感和椰子表面的新鲜质感，使整体色彩看起来更加清晰清爽。

[纹理与色彩氛围]

翡翠色的海面与粉嫩的双颊相映成趣，营造出清爽且充满异国情调的夏日度假氛围。针织上衣的厚实纹理、椰子壳坚硬光滑的质感，以及水润细腻的皮肤质感在明亮的色调中和谐交融。

[胶片、镜头景深与角度]

采用智能手机拍摄般的正面平视特写镜头。利用浅景深效果，使主体面部和前景中的椰子清晰可见，而背景中的海滩和海景则呈现柔和的虚化效果，将焦点完全集中在人物身上。

[背景元素（可选）]

背景是细软的白沙滩，伴有柔和的白色浪花。海洋从清澈透明的翡翠色渐变至深蓝色，背景是湛蓝的夏日天空，地平线附近点缀着淡淡的白云。

[签名] 右下角有小小的 Threads 标志，上方以白色草书字体写着小小的“CHAse”作为签名。9:16

[English]
[인물의 포즈 및 표정]

하얀 모래사장 위에 쪼그려 앉아 한 손으로 커다란 {argument name="음료" default="초록색 생코코넛"}을 들고 빨대로 음료를 마시는 듯한 포즈를 취하고 있습니다. 카메라를 정면으로 바라보며 입술을 살짝 오므려 볼에 공기가 차있는 귀엽고 생기 넘치는 표정이며, 신비로운 눈망울로 카메라를 응시하고 있습니다.

[인물 의상]

촘촘한 골지 짜임이 돋보이는 두꺼운 끈 형태의 {argument name="의상 색상" default="스카이 블루"} 색상 니트 크롭 탑을 입고 있으며, 하의로는 {argument name="하의 소재" default="린넨"} 소재의 흰색 반바지를 착용했습니다. 전체적으로 청량하고 화사한 여름 분위기를 더했습니다.

[인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일]

머리를 높게 올려 묶은 올림머리 스타일로, 얼굴 옆으로 자연스러운 잔머리가 곡선을 그리며 흘러내립니다. 올림 머리 옆쪽과 허리춤에는 커다란 파스텔 핑크 색상의 입체적인 장미꽃 장식을 달았습니다. 메이크업은 양 볼과 콧등까지 붉고 선명한 핑크빛 블러셔를 넓게 발라 숙취 메이크업 스타일을 연출했으며, 길고 뾰족하게 다듬어진 누드 톤의 긴 네일 아트를 하고 있습니다.

[조명 및 빛 방향]

맑은 날 해변의 강렬하고 화사한 자연광이 인물의 등쪽에서 비춰져 얼굴과 몸 앞쪽에 자연스러운 그림자를 드리웁니다. 밝은 햇빛이 피부의 화사한 광택과 코코넛 표면의 신선한 질감을 선명하게 부각하며, 전체적인 색감을 더욱 맑고 청량하게 살려줍니다.

[질감과 색감 무드]

에메랄드빛 바다와 분홍빛 뺨이 어우러져 청량하고 이국적인 여름 휴양지 무드를 자아냅니다. 니트 탑의 도톰한 짜임 질감, 단단하고 매끄러운 코코넛 껍질의 질감, 그리고 인물의 촉촉하고 매끄러운 피부 질감이 생생하게 살아있어 화사한 조화를 이룹니다.

[필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글]

스마트폰 카메라로 촬영한 듯한 정면 눈높이의 클로즈업 숏입니다. 얕은 피사계 심도를 사용하여 전면에 위치한 인물의 얼굴과 코코넛은 매우 선명하게 포착한 반면, 등 뒤로 펼쳐진 해변과 바다 풍경은 부드럽게 아웃포커싱 처리하여 인물에게 온전히 시선이 집중되도록 했습니다.

[배경 요소 (옵션)]

고운 입자의 하얀 모래사장과 그 뒤로 잔잔하게 밀려와 부서지는 흰 거품 파도가 보이는 해변입니다. 맑고 투명한 에메랄드빛에서 짙은 푸른빛으로 이어지는 그라데이션 바다가 펼쳐져 있으며, 수평선 근처에 흰 구름이 살짝 걸친 청명하고 파란 여름 하늘이 배경을 이루고 있습니다.

```text
[中文]
一张以上传人脸为参考的电影感 35mm 胶片人像，主角拥有 {argument name="hair" default="凌乱的深色卷发"} 和 {argument name="facial hair" default="浅胡茬"}，微微抬头仰望。他佩戴着 {argument name="accessory" default="琥珀色镜片的圆形细框太阳镜"}。场景由戏剧性的明暗对比布光定义，锐利的百叶窗斜影在脸部和颈部投射出金色的条纹光影与深邃阴影。背景是一面带有纹理的深色墙壁，同样捕捉到了条纹图案。色彩基调呈现出暖橙色高光与冷青色阴影的高对比度融合。采用 85mm 镜头低角度拍摄，展现出浅景深、细腻的胶片颗粒感以及忧郁的复古电影色调。

A cinematic 35mm film portrait of a man with the uploaded face as reference, featuring {argument name="hair" default="messy dark curly hair"} and a {argument name="facial hair" default="light beard"}, looking slightly upward. The background is a dark, textured wall catching the same striped pattern. The color palette features a high-contrast mix of warm orange highlights and cool teal-toned shadows. Captured from a low angle with an 85mm lens, showcasing a shallow depth of field, subtle film grain, and a moody, vintage cinematic color grade.

### 📷 例 692：丛林猴子自拍提示词

![丛林猴子自拍提示词](../images/%E4%B8%9B%E6%9E%97%E7%8C%B4%E5%AD%90%E8%87%AA%E6%8B%8D%E6%8F%90%E7%A4%BA%E8%AF%8D.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
[人物姿势与表情] 在构图中，一只体型较大且温顺的猴子似乎正拿着相机进行自拍，人坐在猴子身后的栏杆上，猴子则坐在人的腿上。人物双手比出“V”字手势，嘴巴大张，露出灿烂的笑容，向镜头散发出俏皮而充满活力的能量。正前方的猴子也张大嘴巴，露出牙齿，表情滑稽。 [人物服装] 身穿一件浅白色连衣裙，肩带上有精致的荷叶边装饰。 [人物发型与妆容细节] 刘海中分并自然垂在两侧，后方的头发自然盘起。妆容以鲜艳的粉色嘴唇和干净的眼线为特色，清晰而生动地突出了五官，与大笑的表情相得益彰。 [光照与光线方向] 柔和的自然光透过茂密森林的叶片轻轻漫射。没有刺眼的直射阳光，因此人物和猴子的脸上没有明显的阴影，主体被均匀明亮地照亮，营造出一种光彩照人且自然的抓拍感。 [质感与色彩氛围] 镜头前真实地呈现了猴子褶皱且丰富的灰褐色皮毛质感，与人物光滑的皮肤形成对比。背景中清新的绿色灌木、白色的服装以及黄色的手镯，共同营造出一种生动且充满异国情调的自然度假氛围。 [胶片与相机镜头景深、角度] 使用 14mm 超广角镜头拍摄的低角度自拍，光圈 f/2.8，ISO 200，快门速度 1/250 秒。由于超广角镜头的畸变特性，前景中猴子的手和脸显得非常大且具有立体感；得益于深景深，从紧随其后的人物到远处的背景树木都相对清晰地被捕捉到。 [背景元素（可选）] 一个充满茂密野生灌木和巨大热带树木的天然国家公园或森林。人物坐着的地方是带有少量青苔的深色石栏杆或长椅，地面上散落着枯叶，营造出一种置身于深山野林的真实临场感。 [签名] 右下角有 Meta 运营的 Threads 小图标，图标上方用白色草书写着“CHAse”作为小签名。

[English]
[인물의 포즈 및 표정] 길들여진 큰 원숭이가 직접 카메라를 잡고 셀카를 찍는 듯한 구도에서, 인물은 원숭이 바로 뒤에 난간에 앉아 있고 원숭이는 인물의 허벅지 위에 앉아 있습니다. 인물은 양손으로 브이(V) 포즈를 그리며 입을 크게 벌리고 환하게 웃고 있으며, 카메라를 향해 장난기 가득하고 역동적인 에너지를 발산하고 있습니다. 바로 앞의 원숭이 역시 입을 크게 벌려 이빨을 드러내며 익살스러운 표정을 짓고 있습니다. [인물 의상] 어깨 끈에 잔잔한 프릴 장식이 들어간 가벼운 화이트 컬러의 원피스를 입고 있습니다. [인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일] 앞머리를 가르마 타서 양옆으로 내추럴하게 늘어뜨리고, 뒷머리는 자연스럽게 묶어 올린 스타일입니다. 메이크업은 입 안이 다 보일 정도로 활짝 웃는 표정에 맞추어 선명한 핑크빛 립과 깔끔한 아이라인으로 이목구비를 또렷하고 생기 있게 강조했습니다. [조명 및 빛 방향] 울창한 숲속의 나뭇잎 사이로 은은하게 분산되어 들어오는 부드러운 자연광입니다. 강한 직사광선이 없어 인물과 원숭이의 얼굴에 날카로운 그늘이 생기지 않으며, 전체적으로 밝고 균일하게 피사체들을 밝혀주어 화사하고 자연스러운 스냅숏 느낌을 줍니다. [질감과 색감 무드] 원숭이의 자글자글하고 풍성한 회갈색 털 질감이 카메라 바로 앞에서 매우 사실적으로 표현되며, 인물의 매끄러운 피부와 대조를 이룹니다. 배경의 싱그러운 초록빛 수풀과 인물의 화이트 의상, 노란색 팔찌가 어우러져 활기차고 이국적인 자연 속 휴양지 무드를 자아냅니다. [필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글] 초점 거리 14mm 초광각 렌즈, 조리개 값 f/2.8, 감도 ISO 200, 셔터 스피드 1/250초로 설정하여 촬영한 셀카 구도의 로우 앵글 숏입니다. 초광각 렌즈 특유의 왜곡 효과로 인해 전면의 원숭이 손과 얼굴이 매우 크고 입체적으로 부각되어 있으며, 깊은 피사계 심도로 인해 바로 뒤의 인물부터 먼 배경의 나무들까지 비교적 선명하게 포착되었습니다. [배경 요소 (옵션)] 빽빽하고 울창한 야생 수풀과 거대한 열대 나무들이 가득 찬 자연 국립공원 또는 숲속입니다. 인물이 앉아 있는 바닥은 이끼가 살짝 낀 듯한 어두운 석조 난간이나 벤치이며, 바닥에는 마른 나뭇잎들이 흩어져 있어 깊은 자연 속의 생생한 현장감을 줍니다. [싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "CHAse"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다.
```

### 📷 例 697：太空女仆咖啡厅零重力肖像

![太空女仆咖啡厅零重力肖像](../images/%E5%A4%AA%E7%A9%BA%E5%A5%B3%E4%BB%86%E5%92%96%E5%95%A1%E5%8E%85%E9%9B%B6%E9%87%8D%E5%8A%9B%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
主題：
{argument name="主題" default="宇宙メイドカフェへようこそ"}

人物・表情：
{argument name="人物" default="日系若い女性"}、やわらかなブラウンのロングヘア、前髪あり、髪が無重力でふわりと広がっている。ナチュラルメイク、透明感のある肌、ほんのり血色のある頬、やさしく微笑む表情、少し照れたように片手を頬の近くに添える可愛い仕草。

服装・ポーズ：
{argument name="服装" default="黒白ベースの日本風メイドカフェ衣装"}、白いレースのヘッドドレス、ピンクのリボン、ハートモチーフ、フリルの多い白いエプロン、少しフィット感のある上品なデザイン、白いニーハイソックス、黒いストラップシューズ。無重力空間でふわりと浮かび、片手で銀色のトレーを持ち、膝を軽く曲げた可愛い浮遊ポーズ。

背景・光：
宇宙ステーション内のメイドカフェ、大きな窓の外にはひとつだけ見える地球と深い星空。室内は清潔感のある金属フレームと柔らかな照明。周囲にはいちごパフェ、メロンソーダフロート、いちごショートケーキ、たい焼き、紅茶、マカロン、ティーポットが無重力で浮かんでいる。小物は多すぎず、画面全体が見やすく整理されている。

構図・カメラ：
縦長スマホ比率、全身が入る浮遊ポートレート、人物を中央に配置、窓枠で奥行きを作る構図。35mmレンズ、やや広角のダイナミックな視点、浅すぎない被写界深度、前後に浮かぶスイーツで無重力感を強調。地球は背景にひとつだけ配置し、重複させない。

質感・スタイル：
高精細、4K品質、リアルな写真風AI画像、自然な肌の質感、毛穴や髪の細部が自然に見える、過度に加工されていない写真感、日系ポートレート、シネマティックなライティング、透明感のある色調、宇宙カフェの幻想感、スイーツは艶やかで新鮮、いちごはみずみずしく、クリームは柔らかく、メロンソーダは炭酸の泡と氷が見える、雑誌写真のような仕上がり。

ネガティブ：
低画質、不自然な顔、不自然な手、余分な指、欠けた指、歪んだ指、奇妙な目線、左右非対称な目、崩れた顔、歪んだ体、破綻した服、過度な露出、地球が複数ある背景、重複した惑星、不自然な窓枠、浮遊物の重なりすぎ、散らかりすぎた小物、歪んだトレー、崩れたスイーツ、文字化け、ロゴ、透かし、AI特有の不自然な質感
```

[English]
Create a casual vertical smartphone mirror/selfie-style bedroom photo of a young woman posing seated on a bed, face hidden by a large centered square pixelated/blur censor block in a warm beige skin-tone gradient. She has {argument name="hair style" default="dark messy twin buns with loose strands"}, elbows tucked in and both hands cupping her cheeks in a cute morning pose. Overlay the photo with a hand-drawn magical-girl sailor outfit inspired by Sailor Moon: a white top with bold blue sailor collar and shoulder stripes, a huge red chest bow outlined in thick white and black marker strokes, a yellow crescent moon brooch with a small red heart at the center, a blue pleated skirt at the bottom edge, and a matching yellow crescent moon belt ornament. The doodle overlay should look like marker art drawn directly on the photo, with rough sketch lines, white sticker-like borders, blue/red/yellow fill scribbles, and imperfect playful proportions. Surround the subject with exactly 5 red heart doodles, 7 yellow star doodles, 5 small white sparkle doodles, and 2 yellow crescent moon emblems including the chest and waist ornaments. Background: dim indoor bedroom with beige walls, a door and hallway behind her, rumpled white bedding on the right, a chair edge on the left, and a small cluttered table in the back; use realistic flash-photo lighting and a slightly tilted candid composition. Keep the image playful, cute, and homemade, like a social media post about transforming into {argument name="character inspiration" default="Sailor Moon"}.

### 📷 例 703：单色微距皮肤纹理肖像

![单色微距皮肤纹理肖像](../images/%E5%8D%95%E8%89%B2%E5%BE%AE%E8%B7%9D%E7%9A%AE%E8%82%A4%E7%BA%B9%E7%90%86%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

```text
[中文]
一张超精细的 8K 黑白微距肖像，主体为 {argument name="subject" default="年轻男子"}，镜头紧凑地聚焦于他的眼睛、颧骨和嘴唇。明亮的阳光分裂成柔和、波浪状的发光线条，像水下倒影一样在他的皮肤上荡漾。光线呈对角线流动，以梦幻般的图案包裹着面部轮廓，阴影消融在平滑的中灰色调中。皮肤呈现出摄影级的细节：清晰可见的毛孔、淡淡的雀斑、细微的胡茬，以及捕捉着微光的自然纹理。嘴唇被柔和地高亮，眼睛反射出光波的碎片。微距镜头构图，极浅的景深：只有一条被照亮的皮肤纹理清晰可见，其余部分融化在天鹅绒般的模糊中。流动的光线赋予了画面一种扎根于写实主义的超现实玻璃质感。背景渐变为柔和的灰色。画面叠加了细腻的电影质感颗粒。这是一幅亲密且富有情感感染力的单色肖像。

[English]
An ultra-detailed 8K black and white macro portrait of a {argument name="subject" default="young man"}, zoomed in tight on his eye, cheekbone, and lips. Bright sunlight splits into soft, wavy luminous lines rippling across his skin like underwater reflections. The light flows diagonally, wrapping around facial contours in dreamlike patterns, with shadows dissolving into smooth mid-gray tones. Skin rendered in photographic detail: visible pores, faint freckles, slight stubble, natural texture catching glowing light. Lips softly highlighted, eye reflecting fragments of luminous waves. Macro camera framing, razor-thin depth of field: one sharp ridge of illuminated skin, the rest melting into velvety blur. The flowing light gives a surreal, glassy aesthetic grounded in photorealism. Background fades into soft gray gradients. Fine cinematic grain overlays the frame. Intimate, emotionally evocative monochrome portrait.
```

logo、真实球星名字和可识别商业品牌。可以使用抽象旗帜、彩色看台、足球、绿茵场、街头大屏、球迷围巾、复古球衣号码、运动配饰来表达世界杯氛围。画面质感要求真实摄影感，保留自然皮肤纹理、真实毛孔、自然阴影，不要过度磨皮，不要 AI 娃娃脸。光影要有层次，色彩鲜活但高级，带轻微胶片颗粒、轻微过曝、闪光灯质感和夏日空气感。构图为 3:4 竖构图，像高预算时尚杂志写真，不要廉价影楼风，不要 cosplay 杂志封面近景图：画面为半身到近景构图，背景是模糊的彩色旗帜、足球网、银色金属帘和蓝天元素，整体像 2000 年代 Kpop + 体育杂志封面。人物穿复古足球球衣或运动背心，脖子上挂着银色链条和球迷围巾，头发做出 Kpop 风造型：自然蓬松、湿发碎发、彩色小发夹或发带。妆面重点是清透底妆、亮片眼影、睫毛、健康腮红和果冻唇。必须保留真实皮肤纹理，不能磨成塑料脸。人物手里拿着足球靠近脸侧，或者用手轻轻压住球迷围巾。眼神看镜头，表情自信、漂亮、带一点俏皮和攻击性，像世界杯特别企划封面人物。光线为正面闪光灯加自然光，脸部清晰，背景稍微过曝。画面有复古胶片颗粒、Y2K 高饱和色彩、杂志封面质感和真实摄影感。09｜黄昏终场收束图：场景是黄昏后的空足球场，天空有橙粉色晚霞，远处看台和旗帜被虚化，草坪上散落足球、围巾和水瓶。氛围像世界杯夏日比赛结束后的安静一刻。人物坐在草坪上或球场边缘，穿复古球衣外套，里面是运动背心，下身是牛仔短裤、运动短裤或宽松裤装。头发略微被汗水和夏天湿气打湿，妆面还保留亮片和汗水光泽，整体有真实运动后的疲惫和明亮感。人物一只手撑在身后，另一只手抱着足球或搭在膝盖上，抬头看向远处晚霞，不一定看镜头。表情放松、满足、安静，但仍然有生命力，像夏天最热烈的一天结束了。光线为黄昏金橙色逆光，人物边缘有漂亮轮廓光，草坪和皮肤有温暖高光。画面要有复古胶片感、轻微漏光、柔和颗粒、青春电影结尾感。
```

### 📷 例 706：受限人形生物快照

![受限人形生物快照](../images/%E5%8F%97%E9%99%90%E4%BA%BA%E5%BD%A2%E7%94%9F%E7%89%A9%E5%BF%AB%E7%85%A7.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
创作一张昏暗、杂乱室内环境下的抓拍式闪光灯照片，风格仿照 21 世纪初的数码相机拍摄。场景中展示了一个极其瘦削、无毛、外貌苍老的人形生物，它有着光泽的棕褐色皮革状皮肤，坐在一把带有棕色靠背和花纹坐垫的金属框架折叠躺椅上。该生物拥有一个超大的椭圆形头部，但其面部被一个 {argument name="censor block color" default="中棕色"} 的实心方形遮挡块覆盖；其胸腔、肌腱、骨感的双臂、修长的双腿、巨大的双脚以及褶皱的纹理清晰可见。姿势：随意坐着，一条腿叠在另一条腿上，左臂横跨躯干，右臂举在头部旁边，手掌张开，仿佛在挥手。背景为一面带有污渍的灰白色墙壁和灰色混凝土地面，采用强烈的直射闪光灯，阴影浓重，对比度低，具有轻微的颗粒感和业余纪实感。在最左侧的地板上放置一块黑色反光长方形板。在右侧，放置一张小型复古餐桌，配有灰色金属边缘和绿色的底座支架。桌面需展示七个可见物品：一个带有动物插画的白色圆柱形容器、其后方的一个彩色盒子、后部一张小型相框或支撑着的动物照片、一个深色岩石状物体、一个小型深色长方形物体、一个透明玻璃花瓶，以及花瓶中一束红橙色的花。保持构图为横向，以坐着的人形生物为中心，桌子在右边缘处进行裁剪。不要添加任何文字、标志、额外人物或家具。使用 {argument name="photo style" default="强烈的机顶闪光灯业余快照"}，营造出一种平凡、不安且具有纪实感的氛围。

[English]
Create a candid flash-photography snapshot in a dim, cluttered indoor room, styled like an early-2000s digital camera photo. The scene shows one extremely thin, hairless, elderly-looking humanoid figure with glossy tan-brown leathery skin sitting in one metal-framed folding lounge chair with a brown backrest and patterned cushion. The figure has an oversized oval head but its face is covered by a solid square censor block in {argument name="censor block color" default="medium brown"}; its ribcage, tendons, bony arms, long legs, large feet, and wrinkled texture are highly visible. Pose: seated casually with one leg crossed over the other, the left arm resting across the torso, and the right arm raised beside the head with an open hand as if waving. The setting is a plain off-white stained wall and gray concrete floor, with harsh direct flash, strong shadows, low contrast, and slightly grainy amateur realism. Include exactly one black reflective rectangular panel leaning on the floor at the far left. On the right, include exactly one small retro dinette table with a gray metal edge and greenish pedestal legs. On the tabletop show exactly seven visible objects: one white cylindrical container with an animal illustration, one colorful box behind it, one small framed or propped animal photo at the back, one dark rock-like object, one small dark rectangular object, one clear glass vase, and one bouquet of red-orange flowers in the vase. Keep the composition horizontal, centered on the seated figure, with the table cropped at the right edge. Do not add text, logos, extra people, or additional furniture. Use {argument name="photo style" default="harsh on-camera flash amateur snapshot"} and make the room feel mundane, unsettling, and documentary.
```

### 📷 例 713：未来感奢华腕表广告

![未来感奢华腕表广告](../images/%E6%9C%AA%E6%9D%A5%E6%84%9F%E5%A5%A2%E5%8D%8E%E8%85%95%E8%A1%A8%E5%B9%BF%E5%91%8A.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：为 {argument name="brand ambassador" default="David Beckham"} 创作一个电影级的奢华腕表广告概念，将高端男性时尚肖像与未来感制表实验室及超大机械产品特写镜头相结合。

画布：16:9 宽幅横幅，深金属色调，超精细写实 CGI，光泽黑铬，蓝灰色反射，高对比度摄影棚灯光，浅景深。

布局：将男性大使置于画面中心偏左位置，从大腿中部向上截取，身着深海军蓝定制西装，搭配白色开领衬衫和白色方巾，双手可见纹身并扣住西装，左手佩戴黑色奢华腕表。他的面部刻意被 2 个实心遮挡块遮盖：1 个较大的暖棕色正方形遮住上半脸/头部区域，1 个较小的深蓝灰色长方形遮住左下脸区域。在右半部分，展示 1 个巨大的悬浮方形枕形钛金属表壳，透视角度，圆角设计，厚实的抛光表圈，侧面表冠，透明镂空表盘，陀飞轮式圆形机械结构，金色圆环，铬合金桥板，紫色宝石中心，底板上刻有“TITANIUM”字样。

背景：未来感腕表设计工作室，配有分层玻璃面板、弧形透明展示舱、机器人机械装置、精密工具、反射效果、蓝色全息 UI 线条以及深色工业奢华氛围。在右下角加入一个小型的制表师场景：1 名身穿白大褂的技术人员坐在冷蓝色灯光下的工作台前，在微型机械零件旁组装腕表。

需包含的可见产品与设计元素：共 4 个显眼的腕表相关物体：1 个位于右侧的巨大悬浮镂空腕表，1 个大使佩戴的腕表，1 个展示在左侧玻璃模块内的小型腕表，以及 1 个位于右下角工作台上的小型机械组装腕表。

文字内容：添加 7 个清晰可见的文字元素，采用纤细的大写未来感无衬线字体：1) 左上角的“{argument name="headline name" default="DAVID BECKHAM"}”，2) 其下方的“{argument name="tagline" default="TIMELESS DESIGN"}”，3) 嵌入左侧玻璃面板内并带有细引导线的“CUSTOM MODULAR DESIGN”，4) 沿最左侧展示边缘垂直排列的“TITANIUM”，5) 巨型腕表上雕刻的“TITANIUM”，6) 中右下方的“AUTOMATIC MOVEMENT”，以及 7) 右下角的“{argument name="model code" default="DB 01"}”。

风格限制：高端汽车风格的钟表广告，深沉的反射效果，清晰的微机械细节，逼真的西装面料和皮肤纹身，无额外 Logo，无水印，除指定的 7 个文字元素外不包含任何额外文字。

[English]
Goal: Create a cinematic luxury watch advertisement concept for {argument name="brand ambassador" default="David Beckham"}, blending a high-fashion male portrait with a futuristic watchmaking laboratory and oversized mechanical product hero shot.

Canvas: Wide 16:9 banner, dark metallic palette, ultra-detailed photorealistic CGI, glossy black chrome, blue-gray reflections, high contrast studio lighting, shallow depth of field.

Layout: Place the male ambassador slightly left of center, cropped from mid-thigh upward, wearing a tailored dark navy suit, white open-collar shirt, white pocket square, visible tattooed hands clasping the jacket, and a black luxury wristwatch on his left wrist. His face is intentionally obscured by exactly 2 solid censor blocks: 1 large warm brown square over the upper face/head area and 1 smaller dark blue-gray rectangle over the lower-left face area. On the right half, show 1 enormous floating square-cushion titanium watch case angled in perspective, with rounded corners, thick polished bezel, side crown, transparent skeleton dial, tourbillon-like circular mechanism, gold rings, chrome bridges, purple jewel center, and the engraved word “TITANIUM” on the bottom plate.

Background: Futuristic watch design studio with layered glass panels, curved transparent display pods, robotic machinery, precision tools, reflections, blue holographic UI lines, and dark industrial luxury ambience. Include a small watchmaker scene in the lower right: exactly 1 technician in a white coat seated at a workstation under cool blue light, assembling a watch beside miniature mechanical components.

Visible product and design elements to include: exactly 4 prominent watch-related objects: 1 giant floating skeleton watch on the right, 1 wristwatch worn by the ambassador, 1 smaller watch displayed inside the left glass module, and 1 small mechanical watch assembly on the lower-right workbench.

Text content: Add exactly 7 visible text elements in thin uppercase futuristic sans-serif typography: 1) “{argument name="headline name" default="DAVID BECKHAM"}” at top left, 2) “{argument name="tagline" default="TIMELESS DESIGN"}” directly below it, 3) “CUSTOM MODULAR DESIGN” stacked inside the left glass panel with a fine leader line, 4) vertical “TITANIUM” along the far-left display edge, 5) engraved “TITANIUM” on the giant watch, 6) “AUTOMATIC MOVEMENT” near the lower center-right, and 7) “{argument name="model code" default="DB 01"}” at bottom right.

Style constraints: Premium automotive-inspired horology advertisement, moody reflections, crisp micro-mechanical detail, realistic suit fabric and skin tattoos, no extra logos, no watermark, no additional text beyond the 7 specified text elements.
```

**来源：** [@Cherry 2.O](https://x.com/Mind_Boticni/status/2063666107203097076) | 2026-06-07

### 📷 例 718：赛博朋克霓虹轮廓光肖像

![赛博朋克霓虹轮廓光肖像](../images/%E8%B5%9B%E5%8D%9A%E6%9C%8B%E5%85%8B%E9%9C%93%E8%99%B9%E8%BD%AE%E5%BB%93%E5%85%89%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

```text
[中文]
超精细电影感数字肖像，描绘了一位留着凌乱 {argument name="hair color" default="黑色"} 头发和轻微胡茬的年轻男子，采用低角度视角，展现出自信与随性的态度。黑白单色调，醒目的 {argument name="glow color" default="霓虹红"} 轮廓光勾勒出头发、肩膀和面部轮廓。深黑色背景，戏剧性的高对比度光影，锐利的阴影和光泽高光。头顶架着墨镜，黑色衬衫微敞，内搭简约白色 T 恤，佩戴极简银色吊坠项链。眼神深邃直视镜头，表情放松，下颌线分明，充满时尚杂志美学。

现代图像小说插画风格，超洁净矢量渲染，平滑渐变，细节极其锐利，高级海报设计，奢华街头服饰氛围，赛博朋克风格的红色光效点缀，电影级构图，影棚级灯光，极其精细的面部特征，大师级品质，8K 分辨率，暗黑忧郁氛围，ArtStation 热门趋势，戏剧性对比，居中构图，黑色背景。

[English]
Ultra-detailed cinematic digital portrait of a young man with tousled messy {argument name="hair color" default="black"} hair and light stubble, viewed from a low-angle perspective, projecting confidence and effortless attitude. Black and white monochrome color palette with striking {argument name="glow color" default="neon red"} rim lighting outlining the hair, shoulders, and facial contours. 创作一张动态的动漫风格电影感动作插画，展示 {argument name="team name" default="Gen"}：三名时尚的都市雇佣兵站在未来感十足的玻璃钢结构城市十字路口，均以戏剧性的强制透视视角直接向观众举起黑色手枪瞄准。构图采用低机位略微倾斜的广角镜头，左侧角色最近且最大，中间角色稍靠后，右侧角色处于中距离；枪管应突出、呈现透视缩短效果，并居中对准观众。包含 3 名角色：1) 左侧是一名高个子年轻男性，留着凌乱的深青色头发，小麦色皮肤，钴蓝色敞开式外衣内搭黑色背心，穿着深色长裤和腰带，右手向前伸出持枪；2) 中间是一名身材健美的年轻女性，留着深色卷发，扎着高双丸子头或厚卷发，身穿蓝色短款夹克内搭黑色露脐上衣，穿着深色高腰短裤，配有腰带和链条装饰，向前持枪瞄准；3) 右侧是一名高个子年轻女性，留着蓬松的波浪状 {argument name="hair color" default="purple"} 头发，身穿深蓝色无袖战术服装，带有绑带、腰带和银色链条细节，向前持枪瞄准。在每个角色的面部添加扁平的长方形遮挡块，总共 3 个面部遮挡块，采用柔和的棕色调，与原始位置匹配并遮住面部特征。背景是一个密集的近未来大都市，拥有高耸的摩天大楼、数字广告牌、日式赛博朋克氛围、带有零星云朵的明亮蓝天、玻璃反射、包含可读片段（如 {argument name="billboard text" default="SAPIENT"} 和 "NV"）的标牌、街道层面的烟雾、小火苗以及左下角远处的受损车辆。使用丰富的绘画感动漫渲染、锐利的线条、高细节、戏剧性光影、蓝紫色调、强烈的景深、动态能量，且不要添加额外角色。横向 4:3 宽高比。

[English]

The composition is a slightly tilted wide shot from a low camera angle, with the left character closest and largest, the center character slightly behind, and the right character at mid distance; the gun barrels should be prominent, foreshortened, and centered toward the viewer. Include exactly 3 characters: 1) a tall young man on the left with messy dark teal hair, tan skin, open cobalt-blue overshirt over a black tank top, dark pants and belt, extending his right arm forward with a pistol; 2) a shorter athletic young woman in the center with dark curly hair in high twin buns or thick curls, wearing a cropped blue jacket over a black crop top, dark high-waisted shorts with belt and chain accessories, aiming a pistol forward; 3) a tall young woman on the right with voluminous wavy {argument name="hair color" default="purple"} hair, wearing a dark blue sleeveless tactical outfit with straps, belt, and silver chain details, aiming a pistol forward. Add flat rectangular censor blocks over each character’s face, exactly 3 face blocks total, in muted brown tones, matching the original placement and hiding the facial features. The background is a dense near-future metropolis with towering skyscrapers, digital billboards, Japanese cyberpunk atmosphere, bright blue sky with scattered clouds, glass reflections, signage including readable fragments such as {argument name="billboard text" default="SAPIENT"} and "NV", street-level smoke, small fires, and a damaged vehicle far in the lower-left distance. Use rich painterly anime rendering, sharp linework, high detail, dramatic lighting, blue-purple color grading, strong depth, motion energy, and no extra characters. Landscape 4:3 aspect ratio.

### 📷 例 736：暗黑新中式人像摄影

![暗黑新中式人像摄影](../images/%E6%9A%97%E9%BB%91%E6%96%B0%E4%B8%AD%E5%BC%8F%E4%BA%BA%E5%83%8F%E6%91%84%E5%BD%B1.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
请基于我上传的清晰单人人像参考图，生成一组「暗黑系新中式高级写真」，共 9 张，全部为 3:4 竖构图，必须分别生成 9 张单独图片，不要九宫格，不要拼图，不要海报排版。

## 人物一致性
严格保留参考人物本人的真实身份特征：脸型、五官比例、眼睛形状、鼻子、嘴唇、下颌线、发际线、肤色、年龄感、气质和整体辨识度。  
不能换脸，不能变成陌生人，不能网红脸，不能动漫化，不能塑料娃娃脸。  
参考图只用于人物身份，不要保留原照片里的咖啡店、日常衣服、纸杯或生活场景。

## 整体风格
暗黑新中式、黑金东方美学、金箔竹影屏风、暗红地毯、夜色电影感、高级时尚杂志大片。  
画面要冷艳、克制、危险、贵气，有东方悬疑感。  
背景以金箔质感屏风、竹叶投影、黑金边框、深棕、暗红过渡为主。不能大片死黑，不能平涂金色，必须有斑驳肌理、光斑和层次。

## 妆发服装
发型：新中式高盘发或低盘发，带松弛碎发，发髻点缀红色发簪、暗红花饰或细发钗。  
妆容：冷艳时尚妆，上扬黑色眼线、清晰睫毛、微烟熏眼影、干净底妆、鼻梁高光、深红棕或酒红唇色。  
服装：黑色蕾丝改良旗袍，立领、盘扣、收腰、高开衩、蕾丝暗纹、提花纹理清晰。可以露肩颈、手臂和腿部线条，但必须高级克制，不低俗。  
配饰：黑金折扇、细长黑金烟杆、金色几何耳环、金色手镯、红色发簪，少而精。

## 光影与质感
高预算棚拍质感，暖金硬光、侧光、逆光、轮廓光、局部补光结合。  
脸部五官必须清晰，眼神要亮，黑色服装不能死黑，要看见蕾丝、暗纹、褶皱和边缘高光。  
保留真实皮肤纹理、真实身体结构、真实布料和真实光影。  
不要油画感、插画感、3D 建模感、AI 过度磨皮感。  
人物比例自然，手部结构正确，四肢不要畸形。

---

## 01｜金箔竹影全身开场图
全身站姿，人物站在金箔竹影屏风前，身体微侧，一条腿从黑色高开衩旗袍中自然伸出，脚穿黑色细高跟。一只手轻扶屏风边框，另一只手自然垂落或轻扶腰侧。看向镜头，眼神冷静、疏离、有压迫感。暖金硬光 + 侧轮廓光，背景有竹影和暗红深棕过渡。

## 02｜黑金折扇遮面近景图
半身到 3/4 身构图。人物手持黑金刺绣折扇，折扇半展开，轻遮下半张脸，只露出清晰冷艳的眼神。眼神直视镜头，冷淡、清醒、略带挑衅。折扇金线、扇骨、黑色蕾丝旗袍细节清晰。面部柔和提亮，背景竹影硬光。

## 03｜烟杆冷艳特写
上半身近景特写。人物手持细长黑金烟杆，烟杆轻靠唇边或下巴，动作优雅克制，不要夸张烟雾。她微微偏视镜头外，表情冷淡疏离，像有隐秘心事。侧面暖金硬光打出脸部明暗关系，唇色深红棕，蕾丝领口和盘扣清晰。

## 04｜折扇坐姿造型图
人物坐在暗红地毯或低矮座位上，身体微微后靠，单腿自然曲起，手中抱着一把大号黑金刺绣折扇，折扇横在胸前或膝前。眼神看向镜头，冷淡有距离感。突出肩颈、锁骨、手臂和腿部线条，姿态高级，不低俗。背景为金箔竹影屏风。

## 05｜侧身回望烟杆图
中景侧身站姿。人物身体侧向镜头，脸部回望镜头，一只手持细长黑金烟杆横在肩颈附近，另一只手轻扶腰侧。旗袍强调腰线、臀线和侧开衩设计。前景可有虚化黑色屏风边框，形成前景、中景、背景层次。逆侧光勾勒脸部、肩线和腰线。

## 06｜靠墙低头情绪图
人物靠近金箔竹影墙面，一只手轻触墙面或屏风边框，另一只手自然垂下。她微微低头，不看镜头，眼神向下或侧下方，表情安静、冷淡，带一点疲惫和故事感。整体是电影剧照感，不是性感挑逗。背后暖光 + 面部柔和补光，黑色旗袍保留细节。

## 07｜强光压迫感站姿图
全身强气场站姿。人物站在金箔竹影屏风中央，身体略微后靠，一只手扶腰或轻握黑金折扇，另一只手自然垂落。她直视镜头，不笑，眼神清醒、锋利、有压迫感。正面控制面光 + 左侧强硬光 + 背景竹影投射，画面像高级时尚封面候选图。

## 08｜低机位腿部线条图
略低机位，3/4 身到全身构图。人物一条腿从旗袍开衩中自然伸出，另一条腿稳定支撑，身体轻微 S 形转折。一只手持黑金烟杆垂在身侧，另一只手轻触墙面或抬至肩颈附近。表情冷淡疏离，身体比例自然，不要过度拉长。背后强暖光 + 侧面轮廓光。

## 09｜封面级收束图
正式、极简、强烈的封面级收束照。人物站在黑金竹影背景中央，是唯一视觉中心。一只手持黑金刺绣折扇垂于腿侧，另一只手轻扶腰间或自然放松。直视镜头，眼神冷静、克制、自我掌控感强。主面光 + 侧轮廓光 + 背景竹影硬光，黑、金、深棕、暗红之间有细腻过渡。

## 统一禁止
不要九宫格，不要拼图，不要文字，不要水印，不要 logo。  
不要死黑背景，不要廉价影楼风，不要 AI 娃娃脸，不要油画感，不要插画感，不要 3D 感。  
不要多余人物，不要畸形手指，不要奇怪四肢，不要过度拉长身体。
```

**来源：** [@麻酱AI实验室](https://x.com/zhongying14/status/2063599856741187982) | 2026-06-07

```text
[中文]
目标：创作一张充满情绪感的编辑部时尚摄影联系样张，主角为 {argument name="character name" default="一位匿名年轻男模"}，包含多张不同剪裁的肖像，面部由扁平的遮挡块刻意覆盖。

画布：16:9 宽画幅，约 1200×675 像素，排列为整洁的 3×3 网格，面板间留有厚实的白色间隙。全程使用柔和的影棚灰背景、电影级低调照明、浅景深、真实的皮肤纹理以及暗调去饱和的色彩分级。

布局：使用 9 个矩形照片面板，由白色网格线分隔。各面板从不同角度和距离展示同一模特：
1. 左上角面板：近景肖像剪裁，左侧前景有模糊的手部，可见头部侧面和耳朵，面部被一个大的暖棕色矩形遮盖。
2. 上方中间面板：正面半身肖像，可见凌乱短发、耳朵和颈部，面部被一个大的深棕色矩形遮盖。
3. 右上角面板：黑白侧面肖像，模特手触面部，可见耳朵和下颌线，面部被一个大的炭灰色矩形遮盖。
4. 中间左侧面板：身着黑色无袖上衣的背部四分之三侧身视角，有斜向模糊的前景遮挡，面部/头部上方区域被一个垂直的棕色模糊块遮盖。
5. 中间中心面板：以灰色背景为衬的居中近景肖像，可见一只耳朵和头发，面部被一个大的中棕色矩形遮盖。
6. 中间右侧面板：身着黑色无袖上衣的四分之三坐姿/倚靠视角，可见肩膀和上臂，面部被一个较小的暖棕色矩形遮盖。
7. 左下角面板：双手在颈部和锁骨附近交叠的特写，可见黑色无袖服装，不展示面部。
8. 下方中间面板：较宽的侧面坐姿视角，模特身着黑色无袖上衣，肌肉发达的手臂向前伸展，面部被一个小的模糊米棕色方块遮盖。
9. 右下角面板：下半脸、脸颊、下颌胡茬、颈部和肩膀的极近特写，上半脸被剪裁掉或被一个横跨顶部的巨大暖棕色矩形遮盖。

主体细节：模特拥有 {argument name="hair color" default="凌乱的棕色短发"}，肤色浅至中等，可见处有细微的胡茬，体格健壮，身着 {argument name="outfit" default="黑色无袖上衣"}。保持内省且高级的时尚氛围，而非随意感。

视觉风格：写实风格编辑部杂志联系样张，柔和的影棚光，灰色背景，轻微的胶片颗粒感，自然的肤色，浅焦，带有前景模糊，高级时尚摄影质感。在面部区域使用 {argument name="censor block color" default="暖棕色和炭灰色"} 的扁平不透明遮挡块，画面中不包含任何文字、Logo、水印或装饰元素。

约束：保持 9 面板网格、厚白色分隔线以及多样的剪裁方式。遮挡块内不得出现可见的面部特征。确保所有面板在视觉上保持一致，如同同一场拍摄中的同一人。

[English]
Goal: Create a moody editorial fashion photo contact sheet featuring {argument name="character name" default="an anonymous young male model"} in multiple cropped portraits, with the face deliberately obscured by flat censor-like blocks.

Canvas: Wide 16:9 image, approximately 1200×675 px, arranged as a clean 3-by-3 grid with thick white gutters between panels. Use a muted studio-gray background throughout, cinematic low-key lighting, shallow depth of field, realistic skin texture, and a dark desaturated color grade.

Layout: Use exactly 9 rectangular photo panels separated by white grid lines. The panels show the same model from different angles and distances:
1. Top-left panel: close portrait crop with a blurred hand in the foreground on the left, side of head and ear visible, face covered by a large warm brown rectangle.
2. Top-center panel: frontal upper portrait, messy short hair visible, ear and neck visible, face covered by a large dark brown rectangle.
3. Top-right panel: black-and-white side portrait, model touching his face, ear and jawline visible, face covered by a large charcoal-gray rectangle.
4. Middle-left panel: rear three-quarter shoulder view in a sleeveless black top, diagonal blurred foreground obstruction, face/upper head area covered by a vertical brown blur block.
5. Middle-center panel: centered close portrait against gray background, one ear and hair visible, face covered by a large medium-brown rectangle.
6. Middle-right panel: three-quarter seated/leaning view in a sleeveless black top, shoulder and upper arm visible, face covered by a smaller warm brown rectangle.
7. Bottom-left panel: close-up of hands clasped near the neck and collarbone, black sleeveless garment visible, no face shown.
8. Bottom-center panel: wider seated side view, model wearing a sleeveless black top with muscular arms extended forward, face covered by a small blurred beige-brown square.
9. Bottom-right panel: extreme close-up of lower face, cheek, jaw stubble, neck, and shoulder, with the upper face cropped out or blocked by a large warm brown rectangle extending across the top portion.

Subject details: The model has {argument name="hair color" default="short tousled brown hair"}, light-to-medium skin, subtle facial hair/stubble where visible, an athletic build, and wears {argument name="outfit" default="a sleeveless black top"}. Keep the mood introspective and high-fashion rather than casual.

Visual style: Photorealistic editorial magazine contact sheet, soft studio lighting, gray backdrop, slight film grain, natural skin tones, shallow focus, some foreground blur, premium fashion photography. Use flat opaque blocks in {argument name="censor block color" default="warm brown and charcoal gray"} over the face areas, with no text, logos, watermark, or decorative elements.

Constraints: Preserve the exact 9-panel grid, thick white dividers, and varied crops. Do not add visible facial features inside the censor blocks. Keep all panels consistent as one photoshoot of the same person.
```

### 📷 例 801：地中海度假风蕾丝连衣裙人像

```text
[中文]
【人物姿态与表情】她坐在户外餐厅的餐桌旁，转过身，越过肩膀直视镜头。这是一个完美的侧影。她左臂轻撑在桌面上，神情梦幻，眼神平静而迷人，深邃动人。【人物服装】她身穿一件夏季度假风挂脖连衣裙，采用带有精致玫瑰花纹的白色蕾丝面料。露背设计大胆地剪裁至腰线，完美展现出她光滑的背部和纤细的肩膀，营造出精致优雅的海滩度假造型。【人物发型与妆容细节】她的头发整齐地向后梳并扎成高丸子头，露出了颈部和下颌线。妆容强调在阳光下通透、水润、如瓷器般发光的肌肤，淡淡的珊瑚粉色唇妆为双唇增添了自然的活力。【光影与光线方向】地中海的自然阳光从右上方射入，但由于人物上方有针叶树遮挡，阳光并未直射在她身上；取而代之的是透过叶片过滤的光线，强调了明亮感与神秘感。明亮的阳光耀眼地照亮了她的肌肤和白色连衣裙，强烈的直射光在她的背部和桌面形成了自然且立体的阴影。【质感与色彩氛围】大海耀眼的蓝色调、连衣裙的白色以及木质餐桌的棕色形成了强烈的对比。精致编织蕾丝面料的立体质感、光滑的肌肤纹理以及海浪闪烁的质感交织在一起，营造出清新而奢华的度假氛围。【电影与镜头景深、角度】采用正面平视角度，以对角线构图捕捉人物上半身及桌面空间，富有纵深感。使用清晰干净的镜头，将广阔的海景背景收入画面，并调整景深，使远处的游艇和周围道具呈现柔和的虚化效果。【背景元素（可选）】位于阴凉处的户外海边餐厅露台。木桌上摆放着印有英文文本的纸质餐垫、餐盘、皮质菜单和透明玻璃杯。她头顶上方悬挂着如画框般的绿色松枝，背景中，白色的游艇悠闲地漂浮在清澈透明的翡翠色海岸和钴蓝色的大海上。【签名】右下角有一个 Meta 运营的 Threads 小图标，其上方用白色草书小字写着“CHAse”作为签名。3:4 宽高比。

[인물의 포즈 및 표정] 야외 레스토랑의 테이블에 앉아 몸을 돌린 채, 한쪽 어깨 너머로 카메라를 정면으로 응시하고 있습니다. 인물의 완벽한 측면모입니다. 왼팔을 테이블 위에 올려 가볍게 지탱하고 있으며, 차분하면서도 고혹적이고 깊이 있는 눈빛으로 몽환적인 표정을 짓고 있습니다. [인물 의상] 장미 무늬가 섬세하게 짜인 화이트 레이스 소재의 여름 휴양지용 홀터넥 드레스를 입고 있습니다. 등이 허리 라인까지 과감하게 파여 있는 백리스 디자인으로, 인물의 매끄러운 등 라인과 가녀린 어깨선을 아름답게 드러내어 세련되고 우아한 해변 휴양지 룩을 완성합니다. [인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일] 머리카락을 깔끔하게 뒤로 넘겨 높이 묶은 하이번 스타일로 목선과 턱선을 시원하게 드러냈습니다. 메이크업은 햇살 아래서 투명하고 촉촉하게 빛나는 도자기 같은 피부 표현을 강조했으며, 입술에는 자연스러운 생기를 더하는 연한 코랄 핑크빛 립을 발랐습니다. [조명 및 빛 방향] 지중해의 자연 태양광이 우측 상단에서 비추고 있지만 인물의 위쪽으로는 침엽수들이 있어서 직접적으로 태양빛이 인물을 비추고 있지 않으며 나뭇잎 사이로 드문드문 들어오는 빛이 화사함과 신비로움을 강조합니다. 이 화사한 햇빛이 인물의 피부와 화이트 드레스를 눈부시게 밝혀주며, 강한 직사광선으로 인해 등 라인과 테이블 표면에 자연스럽고 입체적인 음영을 형성합니다. [질감과 색감 무드] 눈이 시리도록 푸른 바다의 다채로운 블루 톤과 드레스의 화이트, 나무 테이블의 브라운 색감이 강렬한 대비를 이룹니다. 정교하게 짜인 레이스 원단의 입체적인 질감, 매끄러운 피부 질감, 그리고 반짝이는 바다의 물결 질감이 한데 어우러져 청량하면서도 고급스러운 휴양지 무드를 연출합니다. [필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글] 인물의 상반신과 테이블 공간을 대각선 구도로 입체감 있게 잡아낸 정면 눈높이 앵글입니다. 선명하고 깨끗한 화질의 렌즈를 사용하여 넓은 바다 배경까지 깊이감 있게 담아냈으며, 피사계 심도를 조절하여 먼바다의 요트와 주변 소품들을 부드럽게 아웃포커싱 처리했습니다. [배경 요소 (옵션)] 그늘이 드리워진 해안가 야외 레스토랑의 테라스입니다. 원목 테이블 위에는 영문 글씨가 적힌 종이 매트와 접시, 가죽 메뉴판, 투명한 유리컵이 놓여 있습니다. 머리 위로는 푸른 소나무 나뭇가지가 액자처럼 드리워져 있고, 뒤편으로는 맑고 투명한 에메랄드빛 해안과 코발트블루 바다 위에 흰색 요트가 한가로이 떠 있습니다. [싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "CHAse"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다. 3:4비율

[主体] 参考图像 1 和图像 2。

[姿势与表情]

悠闲地平躺在绿草如茵的山坡上，惬意地仰望天空。一只手臂向镜头方向伸展，营造出强烈的透视感，一只脚微微抬起。氛围自由而宁静。头部完全贴在草地上，以放松的状态注视着天空。

[服装]

身穿一件舒适的波西米亚风格条纹针织开衫，采用 {argument name="clothing colors" default="柔粉色、白色和天蓝色"} 的 3D 混合配色，纽扣解开并向一侧滑落。内搭一件白色吊带背心。下身搭配一条沉稳的灰色迷你裙。一顶干净的白色棒球帽为整体造型增添了运动与休闲的旅行感。

[发型与妆容细节]

头发自然地覆盖在肩部和胸前，部分发丝从帽子下自然流淌在绿草地上。深黑色太阳镜增添了时髦感，突显出高挺的鼻梁和下颌线，皮肤质感清晰可见。

[光影与方向]

来自左上方天空的正午强直射光是主要光源。太阳直接出现在画面中，产生巨大的星芒效果和微妙的镜头光晕，将轮廓和山坡照得明亮通透。

[质感与氛围]

深邃的蓝天与清新的绿草坡营造出强烈的清爽感。针织开衫的毛绒质感与草叶的纹理在畸变的广角构图中形成鲜明对比，营造出充满活力的氛围。

[胶片与相机设置]

使用 f/8.0、1/2000s、ISO 100 以及 {argument name="focal length" default="16mm"} 超广角镜头拍摄。极致的低角度构图，贴近地面仰望天空。深景深确保了从前景的织物细节到远处的云朵都清晰对焦。

[背景元素]

广阔的绿色草坡无尽延伸，远处可见山脊，深蓝色的天空中点缀着朵朵白云。左侧明亮的太阳和右侧山脊上细长的电力塔增添了异域的空间感。

[签名] 右下角有小型的 Threads 标志，上方用白色草书写着“CHAse”。

宽高比 {argument name="aspect ratio" default="9:16"}

[English]
[인물] 이미지 1, 이미지 2참조,

[인물의 포즈 및 표정]

푸른 잔디 언덕 위에 완벽하게 누워 한가로이 하늘을 올려다보고 있는 포즈입니다. 한쪽 팔을 카메라쪽으로 길게 뻗어 극적인 원근감을 연출하고 있으며, 한쪽 발을 살짝 세우고 있습니다. 자유롭고 평온한 분위기가 묻어납니다. 인물은 고개를 들고 있지 않으며 머리는 잔디 바닥에 완벽하게 닿아 있는 편안하고 이완된 상태로 하늘을 바라보고 있습니다.

[인물 의상]

{argument name="의상 색상" default="파스텔 핑크색, 흰색, 스카이 블루 색상"}이 입체적으로 믹스된 포근한 보헤미안 감성의 스트라이프 패턴 니트 가디건을 걸치고 있으며, 단추가 풀어져 옆으로 흘러 내려가 있습니다. 상의 안쪽에는 흰색 끈 나시를 입고 있습니다. 하의로는 차분한 회색 미니스커트를 입고 있습니다. 머리에는 깔끔한 화이트 베이스볼 캡을 눌러써서 스포티하고 캐주얼한 비치 및 트래블 룩을 완성했습니다.

[인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일]

머리카락은 인물의 어깨를 걸쳐 가슴 부분을 차분하게 덮고 있으며, 일부는 모자 아래로 자연스럽게 흘러내려 초록색 잔디밭 위로 부드럽게 펼쳐져 있습니다. 얼굴에는 짙은 블랙 선글라스를 착용하여 시크한 멋을 더했으며, 옆라인의 오뚝한 콧날과 날렵한 턱선, 투명하고 깨끗한 피부 표현이 돋보입니다.

[조명 및 빛 방향]

좌측 상단의 청명한 하늘에서 강렬하게 내리쬐는 한낮의 직사광선이 주 광원입니다. 태양이 렌즈 프레임에 직접 걸치며 화려하고 거대한 햇살 갈라짐 효과와 은은한 렌즈 플레어를 만들어내고 있으며, 인물의 실루엣과 언덕 전체를 눈부시게 밝혀줍니다.

[질감과 색감 무드]

눈이 시리도록 새파란 하늘의 블루 톤과 싱그러운 언덕의 그린 톤이 압도적인 청량감을 자아냅니다. 카메라 바로 앞에 위치한 가디건의 부슬부슬하고 성긴 니트 짜임 질감과 까슬까슬한 풀잎의 질감이 왜곡된 광각 구도 속에서 생생하게 대조되어 생동감 넘치는 무드를 연출합니다.

[필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글]

조리개 f/8.0, 셔터 스피드 1/2000초, ISO 100, 초점 거리 {argument name="초점 거리" default="16mm"} 초광각 렌즈를 사용하여 촬영되었습니다. 인물의 팔등부터 먼 하늘의 태양까지 압도적인 스케일로 포착하기 위해 바닥에 밀착해 하늘을 올려다보는 극단적인 로우 앵글 구도입니다. 특히 인물의 완벽한 옆모습을 담아냅니다. 깊은 피사계 심도를 적용하여 전경의 옷감 디테일부터 원경의 구름까지 전체적으로 선명하게 초점을 맞추었습니다.

[배경 요소 (옵션)]

끝없이 완만하게 내리막으로 이어지는 광활한 녹색 잔디 언덕과 저 멀리 보이는 산의 능선, 그리고 구름이 점점이 떠 있는 깊고 푸른 하늘이 배경을 이룹니다. 하늘 좌측에는 눈부신 태양이 강한 빛을 발하고 있으며, 우측 먼 능선 위에는 가느다란 송전탑 구조물이 서 있어 드넓은 자연 속에 고립된 듯한 이국적인 공간감을 더해줍니다.

[싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "CHAse"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다.

가로세로 비율 {argument name="비율" default="9:16"}
```

### 📷 例 812：梦幻柔和色调的奇幻宝塔

![梦幻柔和色调的奇幻宝塔](../images/%E6%A2%A6%E5%B9%BB%E6%9F%94%E5%92%8C%E8%89%B2%E8%B0%83%E7%9A%84%E5%A5%87%E5%B9%BB%E5%AE%9D%E5%A1%94.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
一幅梦幻般的柔和色调奇幻风景画，采用竖构图。画面展示了一座发光的东亚风格宝塔，坐落在高耸的冰晶山群半山腰，周围环绕着精致的粉色樱花树和飘落的花瓣。场景采用略微仰视的正面广角环境镜头，构图居中，宝塔位于中心偏右位置，左上方伸入一枝樱花，前景层叠着平滑的岩石、发光的瀑布以及带有柔和粉色光迹的倒影池；透视感类似于 35mm 镜头，具有深焦效果和柔和的大气深度，而非强烈的模糊感。将其渲染为高度精致的 3D 奇幻插画，表面极其平滑，岩石呈哑光雕塑质感，山峰晶莹剔透，并带有细腻的体积雾，将空灵的霓虹光感与干净、近乎瓷器的质感相结合。光线柔和且弥散，如同阴天的黄昏，山脉和瀑布散发出冷青蓝色光芒，与宝塔窗户及装饰发出的温暖粉色和桃色光芒形成对比，营造出一种宁静的生物发光感，并带有柔和的光晕和微弱的边缘光。色调以淡冰蓝、薰衣草灰、腮红粉和珍珠白为主，对比度低，质感柔和，氛围宁静而神奇；背景极简且朦胧，没有可见的地平线，使山体仿佛孤立于宁静的梦幻世界中。包含精细的细节，如带有发光轮廓的层叠宝塔建筑、风格化的阶梯和石基、扎根于岩石露台的多棵樱花树、丝滑的流水动态、细小的漂浮花瓣以及水中的细腻倒影。最终图像应呈现出完美且具有电影感的质感，无可见噪点，无硬阴影，光晕极轻，后期处理柔和，呈现出令人联想到高端概念艺术或顶级 CGI 环境设计的发光柔和奇幻美学。

[English]
A dreamy pastel fantasy landscape in a vertical composition, showing a glowing East Asian pagoda-style temple perched halfway up a cluster of towering crystalline ice mountains, surrounded by delicate pink cherry blossom trees and drifting petals. The scene is framed as a wide environmental shot from a slightly low, front-facing angle with a centered composition, the temple placed just right of center, a branch of blossoms entering from the upper left, and a layered foreground of smooth rock forms, luminous cascading waterfalls, and a reflective pool with soft pink light traces; the perspective feels like a 35mm lens with deep focus and gentle atmospheric depth rather than strong blur. Render it as a highly polished 3D fantasy illustration with ultra-smooth surfaces, sculpted matte rocks, translucent icy peaks, and subtle volumetric haze, combining ethereal neon glow with a clean, almost porcelain-like finish. Lighting is soft and diffuse like overcast twilight, with cool cyan-blue emission radiating from the mountains and waterfalls, contrasted by warm pastel pink and peach illumination from the temple windows and accents, creating a serene bioluminescent look with soft bloom and faint edge glow. The color palette is dominated by pale icy blue, lavender-gray, blush pink, and pearly white, with low contrast, high softness, and a calm magical mood; the background is minimal and misty with no visible horizon, making the mountain formation feel isolated in a tranquil dream world. Include fine details such as tiered temple architecture with glowing outlines, stylized stairways and stone base, multiple sakura trees rooted in the rocky terraces, silky water motion, tiny floating petals, and subtle reflections in the water. The final image should feel immaculate and cinematic, with no visible grain, no hard shadows, very light bloom, soft post-processing, and a luminous pastel fantasy aesthetic reminiscent of premium concept art or high-end CGI environment design.
```

### 📷 例 813：电影摄影构图分析帧

![电影摄影构图分析帧](../images/%E7%94%B5%E5%BD%B1%E6%91%84%E5%BD%B1%E6%9E%84%E5%9B%BE%E5%88%86%E6%9E%90%E5%B8%A7.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
创作一张电影感时尚编辑摄影作品，场景位于 {argument name="location" default="明亮的极简卧室"}，并以电影摄影构图分析帧的形式呈现。

主图：一位 {argument name="model" default="年轻女性模特"} 跪在大型窗户旁柔软的白色床上，窗户挂着透明的白色窗帘。她位于画面的左三分之一处，身体与三分法垂直网格线对齐。她身体微微后倾，面向窗户发出的明亮光线，表情平静、疏离且深邃。画面右侧充斥着透过透明窗帘的过度曝光日光所形成的明亮白色光团，与左侧较暗的主体形成平衡。

光影：来自右侧强烈的自然窗光在模特的面部、上半身、腰部和轮廓上形成了高对比度。从左侧添加微妙的辅助补光，以显露阴影区域而不平复对比度。温暖朦胧的清晨氛围，柔和的高光，逼真的皮肤纹理，柔和的阴影，窗帘附近有柔和的光晕。

构图叠加：在图像上添加可见的电影摄影构图指南图形，如同电影学院的分析帧。

包含：
- 细绿色三分法网格线。
- 一条红色垂直线，显示模特身体与左侧三分法网格对齐。
- 绿色圆形焦点标记，分别位于：
  1. 腰部和臀部区域作为焦点 1。
  2. 面部区域作为焦点 2。
  3. 明亮的窗帘/窗户光团作为焦点 3。
- 黄色视线追踪箭头，从面部移动到腰部/臀部，然后指向发光的窗户。
- 红色引导线箭头，沿着床沿和墙板线条指向主要焦点。
- 一条青色箭头，显示来自左侧的辅助补光。
- 画面周围整洁放置的文字标签：
  “身体与三分法网格对齐”
  “高对比度焦点 2”
  “视线追踪”
  “焦点 3”
  “高对比度焦点 1”
  “暗部辅助光源”
  “线条引导至主要焦点”
  “白色光团与三分法网格对齐以平衡画面左侧”

风格：奢华时尚编辑摄影，柔和的电影写实主义，{argument name="color palette" default="暖米色和白色"} 色调，极简卧室，奶油色墙板，白色床上用品，透明窗帘，细微的胶片颗粒感，浅景深，优雅的构图，教育性电影摄影解析叠加。

相机：35mm 镜头，中景广角构图，平视拍摄角度，16:9 宽银幕。

[English]
Create a cinematic fashion editorial photograph in a {argument name="location" default="bright minimal bedroom"}, presented as a cinematography composition analysis frame.

Main Image: A {argument name="model" default="young adult female model"} kneeling on a soft white bed beside a large window with sheer white curtains. She is positioned on the left third of the frame, body aligned with the vertical rule-of-thirds grid line. Her body leans slightly backward, facing toward the glowing window light with a calm, distant, introspective expression. The right side of the image is filled with a large bright white luminous mass from overexposed daylight through sheer curtains, balancing the darker subject on the left.

Lighting: Strong natural window light from the right side creates high contrast on the model’s face, upper body, waist, and silhouette. Add subtle secondary fill light from the left side to reveal shadow areas without flattening the contrast. Warm hazy morning atmosphere, soft highlights, realistic skin texture, gentle shadows, soft bloom near the curtains.

Composition Overlay: Add visible cinematography composition guide graphics over the image, like a film school analysis frame.

Include:
- Rule of thirds grid lines in thin green.
- A red vertical line showing the model’s body aligned on the left rule of thirds grid.
- Green circular focus markers around:
  1. Waist and hip area as Focus Point 1.
  2. Face area as Focus Point 2.
  3. Bright curtain/window mass as Focus Point 3.
- Yellow eye-trace arrows moving from the face to the waist/hip, then toward the glowing window.
- Red leading-line arrows following the bed edge and wall panel lines toward the main focus point.
- A cyan arrow showing secondary fill light coming from the left side.
- Text labels placed cleanly around the frame:
  “Body aligned on the grid of thirds”
  “High contrast Focus Point 2”
  “Eye Trace”
  “Focus Point 3”
  “High contrast Focus Point 1”
  “Secondary light source for dark parts”
  “Lines lead to the main focus point”
  “The white mass is aligned on the grid of thirds to balance the left side of the image”

Style: Luxury fashion editorial photography, soft cinematic realism, {argument name="color palette" default="warm beige and white"} color palette, minimal bedroom, cream wall panels, white bedding, sheer curtains, subtle film grain, shallow depth of field, elegant composition, educational cinematography breakdown overlay.

Camera: 35mm lens, medium wide framing, eye level camera angle, 16:9 widescreen.
```

**来源：** [@BMX](https://x.com/bmx_ai13/status/2064109968203362408) | 2026-06-08

### 📷 例 814：赛博朋克科技美学徽章肖像

![赛博朋克科技美学徽章肖像](../images/%E8%B5%9B%E5%8D%9A%E6%9C%8B%E5%85%8B%E7%A7%91%E6%8A%80%E7%BE%8E%E5%AD%A6%E5%BE%BD%E7%AB%A0%E8%82%96%E5%83%8F.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
竖版徽章式肖像，纯黑背景，深度科技美学。东亚女性，三十岁出头，面容锐利而沉静，眼神微微上挑，仿佛在凝视画框之外的某种深层真相——是一种沉默的洞察力。黑发利落地挽成低发髻，一缕松散发丝在蓝色轮廓光中如数据丝线般若隐若现。不戴眼镜，眼睛是绝对焦点：深色虹膜内环泛着微弱的青蓝生物荧光，暗示深度认知与洞察。着装：剪裁利落的午夜蓝高领长外套，面料上隐约可见几何形接缝线条，如同电路走线；哑光质感中透着若有若无的虹彩光泽。内搭简约深色底衣。除领口一枚极简几何胸针——微型棱镜造型——外无任何饰品。人物包裹于不对称金属边框中：拉丝深色钛金属质感，切角边缘，表面有精密磨损纹理，嵌入若隐若现的青色微光线条，如呼吸般脉动。边框顶部与底部饰有菱形/棱镜装饰元素。背景：一枚巨大的发光深蓝棱镜悬于人物身后，将一束无形之光分解为青色与紫色的细光谱弧线，环绕肖像。外围是带有微妙六边形网格纹理的同心细环，暗合神经网络拓扑结构。柔和的体积光从后方弥散而出，营造深度探索的意境。布光：明暗分割——右侧面部被冷青光照亮（技术、清晰、逻辑），左侧面部隐于深影中，保留一丝微弱的暖调底色（人性、好奇、理智之下的温度）。皮肤呈现毛孔级的写实质感，仿佛皮层之下有某种内在能量微微发光。徽章底部文字 "DS Agent"，精炼的极简无衬线金属质感字体，带有从深蓝到青色的微妙渐变，略微凹陷地嵌入金属底板中。艺术风格：电影级角色概念、智性科幻、暗黑学院融合赛博朋克、高端概念艺术、8K、超写实、丰富材质渲染、体积光照。1:1，圆角矩形
```

**来源：** [@ShyNloc](https://x.com/shynloc/status/2064095516699611203) | 2026-06-08

### 📷 例 819：动漫 Cosplay 镜面自拍

The characters feel like real people in flawless cosplay rather than fully rendered anime figures.

### 📷 例 834：网球少女时尚摄影系列

![网球少女时尚摄影系列](../images/%E7%BD%91%E7%90%83%E5%B0%91%E5%A5%B3%E6%97%B6%E5%B0%9A%E6%91%84%E5%BD%B1%E7%B3%BB%E5%88%97.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
请基于我上传的清晰单人人像参考图，创作一组 8 张「网球女孩」主题时尚写真，全部为 3:4 竖构图，分别独立成片。必须保留参考人物本人的真实五官特征、脸型、眼睛、鼻子、嘴唇、肤色和辨识度，不能换脸，不能变成陌生人，不能网红脸，不能动漫化，不能塑料娃娃脸。皮肤保留真实纹理和实拍感。整体风格为：夏日网球少女、时尚运动大片、Y2K 体育杂志、青春、活力、甜酷、健康性感。画面像高预算杂志写真，不是普通运动照，不是廉价影楼风。服装元素可围绕：白色网球裙、百褶短裙、白色运动背心、短款 polo、网球连衣裙、黄色 V 领针织毛衣、白色运动外套、发带、腕带、白袜、复古网球鞋。发型可用高马尾、自然卷发、半扎发、运动感碎发。妆容清透但精致，健康腮红、亮泽唇、自然睫毛。场景以蓝色网球场、网球网、白色边线、散落网球、球筐、蓝天、看台为主。色彩明亮高级，以蓝色、白色、黄色、荧光绿为主。光线为夏日阳光硬光或正面闪光灯，清晰阴影，轻微胶片感。8 张图的人物表情、眼神、角度、机位、动作必须明显不同，不能雷同。避免眼神空洞、姿势重复、站桩摆拍。动作自然有抓拍感，可包含挥拍、坐姿、躺姿、趴网、抛球、回头、走动、近景造型等。身体比例、手指、四肢、球拍结构必须正常真实。01｜趴网开场图 请生成第 1 张：蓝色户外网球场，人物穿黄色 V 领针织毛衣和白色百褶网球裙，戴发带，身体轻轻趴在网球网上，一只手垂下拿网球拍，另一只手扶住球网。看向镜头，眼神自信、俏皮、灵动。高饱和蓝黄撞色，夏日硬光，时尚运动杂志质感。02｜坐姿甜酷图 请生成第 2 张：人物坐在蓝色网球场地面，穿白色网球裙、白色运动背心、白色运动外套，腿部自然延展，一只手扶着网球拍，另一只手拿网球。表情甜酷、放松、自信。背景有网球网和蓝天，画面明亮，像运动时尚大片。03｜挥拍动态图 请生成第 3 张：人物穿白色无袖网球连衣裙，扎高马尾，在蓝色网球场做真实挥拍动作，裙摆和头发随动作扬起。表情专注，有比赛感。全身或七分身构图，动态抓拍感强，阳光硬光清晰。04｜封面半身图 请生成第 4 张：人物站在网球场或纯蓝背景前，穿白色运动背心和白色外套，露出健康腰腹线条，手举网球拍靠近头顶，另一只手自然抬起或拿球。半身近景构图，眼神直视镜头，冷静、自信、时尚，像体育杂志封面。05｜躺地反差图 请生成第 5 张：人物躺在蓝色球场地面，穿黄色针织毛衣和白色百褶裙，身边散落网球和球拍。镜头俯拍，身体自然舒展，一只手拿网球，一只手搭在球拍上。表情慵懒、俏皮、轻松，画面有明显蓝黄撞色和阳光阴影。06｜球拍前景近景图 请生成第 6 张：人物站在网球网前，穿白色网球裙和蓝白运动外套，手持网球拍靠近镜头形成前景透视，另一只手拿网球靠近脸侧。半身近景，眼神漂亮、自信、有一点胜负欲。闪光灯质感，五官清晰，手和球拍结构正常。07｜回头抓拍图 请生成第 7 张：人物穿白色 polo 网球裙，黄色针织衫披在肩上，背对镜头向前走，在网球场边突然回头看镜头，一只手拿球拍，另一只手整理发带。全身或七分身构图，像被叫住的一瞬间，真实抓拍感，轻松明亮。08｜收束胜利图 请生成第 8 张：训练结束后的球场边，人物穿白色网球裙和黄色针织毛衣，发带略松，头发有一点凌乱和汗感，坐在场边抱着球拍，手里拿着网球。表情满足、放松、带一点胜利后的余韵。画面柔和但明亮，像整组写真的收尾大片。
```

主体数量：恰好 2 位全身女性时尚模特。两人的面部均经过刻意模糊处理，呈现为平滑的米色矩形/椭圆形遮罩，但发型、服装、身体姿势和配饰保持细节清晰。

左侧模特：身着可爱的哥特洛丽塔偶像服装，{argument name="left character style" default="一位留着长款灰金色波浪双马尾、系着深蓝色丝带的年轻女性"}。左侧面板服装清单：1 件海军蓝配米色格子短裙，配白色水手领，领口处有 1 个海军蓝蝴蝶结，1 件带有金色纽扣的修身紧身胸衣，1 条百褶裙，1 条白色蕾丝衬裙，2 个带蝴蝶结的白色腕饰，2 只透明白色花纹过膝袜，2 个深色蝴蝶结袜带，以及 2 双带脚踝蝴蝶结的白色厚底玛丽珍鞋。搭配精致的首饰，如小项链和耳环。姿势害羞可爱，膝盖微内扣，双手提起裙摆边缘。

右侧模特：身着朋克辣妹街头服装，{argument name="right character style" default="一位留着长款白金色蓬松波浪卷发的年轻女性"}。右侧面板服装清单：1 件印有“GLAMOROUS JANE”字样及骷髅插画的黑色长袖上衣，2 只斑马纹袖套，1 条黑色迷你裙，1 条带金属环的铆钉腰带，2 条垂下的银色链条，1 个黑色单肩包，佩戴多款手链和戒指，2 只不对称过膝袜（一只为纯黑色，另一只为黑白条纹），以及 2 双黑色厚底扣带靴。姿势前卫自信，一腿向前，一只手叉腰，另一只手自然放松。

环境：现代日本城市时尚街区人行道，明亮的绿树，阳光照射的路面，玻璃店面的倒影，远处的行人保持微小且不显眼。右侧面板包含一个带有白色抽象 X Logo 的黑色垂直街道横幅，以及类似“CROSS NIGHT”的小字。

视觉风格：2.5D 照片级写实街头摄影与精致 AI Cosplay 插画的混合体，服装纹理清晰，光影写实，高阶时尚街拍构图，色彩鲜艳自然。使用 {argument name="lighting" default="明亮的午间阳光，伴有斑驳的树影"}。

文本内容：包含恰好 2 个可见文本区域：底部中心的小型水印“AI COSPLAY EXPO”以及右侧背景横幅上的白色 X 型 Logo 和微小的“CROSS NIGHT”风格文字。不要添加任何其他可读标题。

约束条件：保持两人的面部模糊且匿名，展示两位角色的全身（从头部到鞋子），保持精确的双面板分割，前景中不得出现额外人物，不得裁切脚部，不得出现动漫面部细节，不得包含原始提示词文本，不得添加边框。

[English]
Goal: Create a high-resolution 2.5D photorealistic fashion generator image showing two full-body AI cosplay fashion models side by side on a sunny urban shopping street, like a street-style lookbook comparison.

Canvas: Landscape 4:3 image, approximately 800×640 px, split vertically into exactly 2 equal panels with no border line. Bright daylight, sharp details, shallow-to-medium depth of field, realistic street photography with a polished AI illustration finish.

Layout: The left panel shows one model standing centered on a sidewalk in front of glass storefronts and trees, holding out the sides of her skirt in a cute pose. The right panel shows one model standing centered on a city sidewalk with glass buildings, trees, street signs, and a black hanging banner in the background, posing confidently with one hand near her hip. Add a small white event-style watermark/logo near the lower center overlapping the split, reading “AI COSPLAY EXPO” with smaller tiny unreadable event text below and a simple white mascot/icon above.

Subject count: Exactly 2 full-body female fashion models. Both have intentionally blurred, featureless faces with smooth beige rectangular/oval blur masks, while hair, clothing, body pose, and accessories remain detailed.

Left model: A cute gothic lolita idol outfit on {argument name="left character style" default="a young woman with long ash-blonde silver wavy twin-tail hair tied with dark blue ribbons"}. Outfit count for the left panel: 1 navy-and-cream plaid short dress with a white sailor collar, 1 navy bow at the neckline, 1 fitted corset-like bodice with gold buttons, 1 pleated flared skirt, 1 white lace underskirt, 2 white wrist cuffs with bows, 2 sheer white patterned thigh-high stockings, 2 dark bow garters, and 2 white platform Mary Jane shoes with ankle bows. Add delicate jewelry such as a small necklace and earrings. The pose is shy and cute, knees slightly inward, both hands lifting the skirt edges.

Right model: A punk gyaru streetwear outfit on {argument name="right character style" default="a young woman with long platinum-blonde voluminous wavy hair"}. Outfit count for the right panel: 1 black graphic long-sleeve top reading “GLAMOROUS JANE” with a skull illustration, 2 zebra-striped sleeves, 1 black mini skirt, 1 studded belt with metal rings, 2 hanging silver chains, 1 black shoulder bag, multiple bracelets and rings, 2 mismatched thigh-high socks where one is solid black and one is black-and-white striped, and 2 black platform buckle boots. The pose is edgy and confident, one leg forward, one hand at the waist, the other relaxed.

Environment: Modern Japanese city fashion district sidewalk, bright green trees, sunlit pavement, reflections in glass storefronts, distant pedestrians kept small and unobtrusive. The right panel includes a black vertical street banner with a white abstract X logo and small text resembling “CROSS NIGHT.”

Visual style: 2.5D hybrid of photorealistic street photography and polished AI cosplay illustration, crisp clothing textures, realistic lighting and shadows, high-fashion street snap composition, vibrant but natural colors. Use {argument name="lighting" default="bright sunny midday lighting with dappled tree shadows"}.

Text content: Include exactly 2 visible text areas: the small lower-center watermark “AI COSPLAY EXPO” and the right-side background banner with a white X-like logo and tiny “CROSS NIGHT”-style wording. Do not add any other readable captions.

Constraints: Keep both faces blurred and anonymous, show both characters full body from head to shoes, maintain the exact two-panel split, no extra people in the foreground, no cropped feet, no anime face details, no raw prompt text, no frame border.

目标：创建一个简洁的横向布光对比表，标题为 {argument name="headline text" default="LIGHTING RESPONSE TESTS"}，展示同一位未来感时尚模特在五种不同摄影棚布光设置下的效果。

画布：16:7 宽幅演示项目，浅灰白色背景，极简主义编辑排版，图像面板间设有细白色间距，底部边缘带有微妙的黑色边框。

布局：将标题置于左上角，使用大写黑色无衬线字体。下方从左至右排列 5 个等大的垂直肖像面板，每个面板中均为同一位全身正面站立的女性模特。在每个面板下方添加两行说明文字，第一行为大写的设置名称，第二行为色温或色彩备注。

主体细节：模特为身材高挑纤细的女性，姿态中性对称，双臂自然垂于身体两侧，头发向后梳起，面部刻意进行柔焦处理或无明显特征。她身着一件光泽感虹彩半透明高级定制礼服：高领、无袖雕塑感金属胸衣、液态塑料质感表面、长款透明斗篷式裙摆垂至地面，珍珠银白色面料带有强烈的镜面高光。在所有五个面板中保持服装完全一致；仅改变布光和背景色调。

五个布光面板，具体要求：
1. 柔光箱 (SOFTBOX) — 日光 / 5600K：明亮的高调白色摄影棚背景，柔和均匀的日光，呈现珍珠般的反光。
2. 轮廓光 (RIM LIGHT) — 冷光 / 6500K：深蓝灰色背景，冷蓝色轮廓光勾勒出模特和裙装边缘，呈现冰冷的高光。
3. 黄金时刻 (GOLDEN HOUR) — 暖光 / 3200K：暖米棕色背景，琥珀色金色照明，透过半透明裙摆呈现柔和的日落余晖。
4. 霓虹城市 (NEON CITY) — 青色 / 品红色：深色城市蓝背景，一侧为强烈的青色光，另一侧为品红色光，礼服上呈现鲜艳的霓虹反射。
5. 低调光 (LOW KEY) — 戏剧性 / 4500K：近乎黑色的背景，戏剧性的低调布光，银色高光与深邃阴影。

视觉风格：照片级真实感的时尚布光测试，光泽反射材质，电影级摄影棚渲染，清晰的全身构图，专业的 Lookbook 对比风格，简洁的排版，无额外 Logo 或水印。使用 {argument name="model outfit" default="虹彩半透明银色高级定制礼服，配有飘逸的透明斗篷裙摆"} 以及 {argument name="model pose" default="正面中性 T 台站姿"}。

[English]
Goal: Create a clean horizontal lighting comparison sheet titled {argument name="headline text" default="LIGHTING RESPONSE TESTS"}, showing the same futuristic fashion model under five different studio lighting setups.

Canvas: Wide 16:7 presentation board on a light gray-white background, minimalist editorial layout, thin white gutters between image panels, subtle black border along the bottom edge.

Layout: Place the title in the upper left in uppercase black sans-serif letters. Below it, arrange exactly 5 equal vertical portrait panels from left to right, each containing the same full-body front-facing female model standing centered. Under each panel, add a two-line caption with the setup name in uppercase and the color temperature or color note below it.

Subject details: The model is a tall slender woman with a neutral symmetrical pose, arms relaxed at her sides, hair pulled back, face intentionally softly blurred or featureless. She wears a glossy iridescent translucent couture gown: high collar, sleeveless sculpted metallic bodice, reflective liquid-plastic surface, long sheer cape-like skirt panels flowing to the floor, pearlescent silver-white fabric with strong specular highlights. Keep the outfit identical in all five panels; only the lighting and background tones change.

Five lighting panels, exactly:
1. SOFTBOX — Daylight / 5600K: bright high-key white studio background, soft even daylight, pale pearly reflections.
2. RIM LIGHT — Cool / 6500K: dark blue-gray background, cool blue rim light outlining the model and dress edges, icy highlights.
3. GOLDEN HOUR — Warm / 3200K: warm beige-brown background, amber golden illumination, soft sunset glow through the translucent skirt.
4. NEON CITY — Cyan / Magenta: dark urban blue background, strong cyan light from one side and magenta-pink light from the other, vibrant neon reflections across the gown.
5. LOW KEY — Dramatic / 4500K: nearly black background, dramatic low-key lighting, silver highlights and deep shadows.

Visual style: Photorealistic fashion lighting test, glossy reflective materials, cinematic studio render, sharp full-body composition, professional lookbook comparison, clean typography, no extra logos or watermarks. Use {argument name="model outfit" default="iridescent translucent silver couture gown with flowing sheer cape panels"} and a {argument name="model pose" default="front-facing neutral runway stance"}.
```

### 📷 例 904：90 年代怀旧地铁胶片人像

[中文]
将上传的人脸参考照片转换为一张自然的电影感 35mm 胶片照片。

人脸是最高优先级的元素。请保留参考照片中人物确切的面部特征、性别、年龄段、种族、面部比例、皮肤纹理、眼睛细节、发型、发色、面部结构以及自然表情。请勿更改或重新诠释人物的性别或标志性面部特征。

画面应呈现出一种仿佛由陌生人在安静的通勤途中随手拍下的感觉。自然的姿态、真实的肢体语言和细腻的情感至关重要。

服装：{argument name="outfit style" default="符合主体性别和风格的写实日常穿搭"}。休闲夹克、毛衣、连帽衫、外套或街头风格服饰。保持造型自然、经典且可信。

光影：柔和的地铁环境光与温暖的金色高光交织。整张图像应呈现出受复古电影和 90 年代胶片摄影启发的独特暖金黄色调。

背景：写实的地铁车厢内部，包含金属质感、窗户、座椅、扶手杆以及微妙的反射。背景应保持柔和的虚化，同时保留可辨识的地铁细节。

氛围：怀旧、电影感、都市、情感丰富且写实。图像应如同 90 年代青春成长电影中遗失的一帧画面。

构图：宽幅电影比例，中景镜头，浅景深，自然取景，焦点清晰地聚焦于面部，而地铁内部保持柔和的虚化。

The face is the highest priority element. Preserve the person's exact facial identity, gender, age range, ethnicity, facial proportions, skin texture, eye details, hairstyle, hair color, facial structure, and natural expression from the reference photo. Do not alter or reinterpret the person's gender or defining facial characteristics.

The subject is naturally adjusting a {argument name="accessory" default="white wired earphone"} in one ear, creating an authentic candid moment.

Wardrobe: {argument name="outfit style" default="realistic everyday fashion appropriate to the subject's gender and style"}. Casual jacket, sweater, hoodie, coat, or streetwear-inspired outfit. Keep the styling natural, timeless, and believable.

Lighting: soft ambient subway lighting mixed with warm golden highlights. The entire image should feature a distinct warm golden-yellow color grading inspired by vintage cinema and 1990s film photography.

Background: realistic subway train interior with metallic textures, windows, seats, poles, and subtle reflections. The background should remain softly blurred while maintaining recognizable subway details.

Atmosphere: nostalgic, cinematic, urban, emotional, and realistic. The image should feel like a lost frame from a 1990s coming-of-age film.

Composition: wide cinematic aspect ratio, medium shot, shallow depth of field, natural framing, razor-sharp focus on the face while the subway interior remains softly blurred.

### 📷 例 1002：摄影棚时尚肖像角色置换

```text

创建一个受上传的蓝色背景参考照片启发的高端摄影棚时尚肖像。重现原始构图，匹配取景、焦距感、相机位置、主体比例、透视关系以及整体视觉平衡，同时保持逼真的摄影质感。

将参考模特替换为所提供角色图像中的人物。保留该角色真实的脸部特征、骨骼结构、肤色、眼型、发型、比例及可辨识的特征。避免任何改变身份的过度美化。面部应保持自然的表情，并忠实于原始角色。

紧密匹配原始的肢体语言：露出上半身，肩膀放松，下巴微抬，双眼看向相机上方，传达出沉静的自信与深思。保持画面中相同的存在感和空间定位。

为角色搭配现代超大廓形街头时尚服饰。使用质感高级的宽松针织衫或卫衣，展现自然的织物垂坠感，叠戴极简主义链条项链，并佩戴轻微透色的眼镜。服装可根据角色进行定制，但应保持相同的奢华超大廓形和时尚大片美学。

保持图像构图紧凑，采用相同的肖像裁剪和主体位置。确保相机到主体的距离以及画面中的视觉权重完全一致。最终效果应与在此特定摄影棚环境中拍摄的专业照片无异。

照片级写实编辑摄影，奢华时尚大片，电影感摄影棚肖像，高级街头风格造型，深色单色蓝色背景，真实皮肤纹理，微妙的电影级对比度，精细的织物渲染，自然光线衰减，商业广告品质，浅景深，杂志封面美学。

负面提示词：

请勿更改背景颜色或摄影棚环境。请勿更改姿势、取景、裁剪、透视、主体距离、光照方向或视线。请勿修改面部身份、面部比例、肤色或发型。避免过度修图、人工磨皮、风格化插画效果、过度锐化、多余配饰、文字叠加、Logo、水印、重复主体或不切实际的服装物理效果。

Create a high-end studio fashion portrait inspired by the uploaded blue-background reference photograph. Recreate the original composition with matching framing, focal length feel, camera position, subject scale, perspective, and overall visual balance while maintaining a realistic photographic appearance.

Replace the reference model with the character from the supplied character image. Preserve the character's authentic facial identity, bone structure, skin tone, eye shape, hairstyle, proportions, and recognizable features. Avoid beautification that alters identity. The face should remain naturally expressive and true to the source character.

Match the original body language closely: upper torso visible, shoulders relaxed, chin slightly elevated, eyes directed upward beyond the camera, conveying quiet confidence and contemplation. Maintain the same sense of presence and spatial positioning within the frame.

Retain the rich cobalt-blue studio backdrop and the refined editorial atmosphere. Reproduce the original lighting setup with soft directional illumination, subtle facial highlights, realistic reflections on eyewear and jewelry, and smooth shadow transitions around the jawline, neck, and clothing folds. Preserve depth, dimensionality, and studio realism.

Use a premium relaxed-fit knit sweater or sweatshirt with natural fabric draping, layered with a minimalist chain necklace and lightly tinted translucent eyewear. The wardrobe may be customized to suit the character, but should maintain the same luxurious oversized silhouette and fashion-campaign aesthetic.

Keep the image tightly composed with the same portrait crop and subject placement. Ensure identical camera-to-subject distance and visual weight in the frame. The final result should feel indistinguishable from a genuine professional photoshoot captured in this exact studio environment.

Photorealistic editorial photography, luxury fashion campaign, cinematic studio portrait, premium streetwear styling, deep monochromatic blue backdrop, realistic skin texture, subtle film-grade contrast, detailed fabric rendering, natural lighting falloff, commercial advertising quality, shallow depth of field, magazine-cover aesthetics.

黑色盘发，呈现出湿润或微湿的质感，自然地向后梳理。几缕细碎、湿润的刘海自然地垂在额头和眼睛旁，增添了纯净感。妆容采用透明且水润的裸妆风格，几乎不着粉黛，保留了清晰的皮肤纹理，仅在唇部点缀了极淡的色彩。

[光影与方向]

梦幻魔幻时刻的柔和夕阳余晖环绕在画面右侧。采用逆光构图，主光源位于人物身后，在肩部轮廓勾勒出微妙的剪影。然而，面部处于深影中，没有对面部进行补光。这是一张如同用智能手机拍摄的写实照片。

[质感与氛围]

左侧半边天空充盈着橙色光芒，紫色的日落倒映在平静的海面上，营造出神秘而浪漫的氛围。右侧略显昏暗。可以感受到低光环境下智能手机摄像头特有的柔和细腻噪点，人物湿润的皮肤和发丝质感增添了舒适的氛围。

[相机景深与角度]

垂直 2 分屏布局。采用正面平视特写镜头，聚焦于胸部以上及面部表情。焦点准确地锁定在主体上，远处的地平线和云层呈现出柔和的虚化效果。人物位于右侧，夕阳位于左侧。

[背景（可选）]

广阔平静的海面上方，展开了一场绚丽壮观的日落。天空被巨大的紫色积云与深橙色交织覆盖，海面如镜面般倒映着天空。在最右侧的地平线尽头，隐约可见岛屿或建筑的剪影。

[签名] 右下角有一个小小的 Threads 标志，上方用白色草书写着“CHAse”。3:4 比例

[English]
[인물] {argument name="인물" default="이미지 1"} 참조.

[인물의 포즈 및 표정]

동일한 구도의 사진 두 장이 위아래로 나란히 배치된 2분할 구성입니다. 두 사진 모두 양손으로 볼을 부드럽게 감싸 쥔 채 카메라 앞에 가까이 다가와 있습니다. 위의 사진에서는 시선을 살짝 우측위로 돌려 아련하고 장난기 어린 표정을 짓고 있으며, 아래의 사진에서는 동그랗고 맑은 눈으로 카메라 정면을 똑바로 응시하며 내추럴하고 사랑스러운 표정을 보여줍니다.

[인물 의상]

목뒤로 얇은 끈을 묶는 스타일의 연한 보라색 홀터넥을 착용하고 있습니다. 오른쪽 쇄골 라인 근처에는 작고 희미한 "CHAse" 레터링 타투가 살짝 새겨져 있어 심플하면서도 확실한 포인트를 줍니다.

[인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일]

물기나 살짝 젖은 듯한 검은 머리칼을 뒤로 자연스럽게 올려 묶은 올림머리 스타일입니다. 가늘고 촉촉한 앞머리 몇 가닥이 이마와 눈가를 따라 자연스럽게 흘러내려 청초한 느낌을 더합니다. 메이크업은 화장기가 거의 없는 투명하고 촉촉한 민낯 스타일로, 맑은 피부 결을 그대로 살리고 입술에는 아주 연한 혈색만 부여했습니다.

[조명 및 빛 방향]

해가 수평선 너머로 저무는 환상적인 매직 아워의 부드러운 노을빛이 프레임 우측공간을 감싸고 있습니다. 주 광원이 인물의 뒤편에 위치한 역광 구도로, 인물의 어깨 라인을 따라 은은한 실루엣이 형성됩니다. 하지만 전체적으로 어두워서 얼굴에는 그림자가 강력하게 드리우며 음영을 만듭니다. 얼굴 방향에는 조명이 전혀 없습니다. 실제 스마트폰 카메라로 그대로 찍은 듯한 사실적인 사진입니다.

[질감과 색감 무드]

프레임 좌측의 하늘을 반쯤 채운 오렌지빛, 그리고 보랏빛 노을이 잔잔한 바다에 그대로 투영되어 신비롭고 낭만적인 무드를 자아냅니다. 프레임 우측에는 약간 어둡습니다. 저조도 스마트폰 카메라 특유의 부드럽고 미세한 노이즈 입자 질감이 느껴지며, 인물의 촉촉한 피부 및 머릿결의 질감이 아늑한 감성을 자아냅니다.

[필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글]

하나의 프레임 안에 상하로 두 개의 컷이 나누어진 세로형 2분할 레이아웃입니다. 인물의 바스트 위의 상반신과 얼굴 표정을 집중해서 보여주는 정면 눈높이의 클로즈업 숏입니다. 피사체인 인물에게 초점이 정확히 맞추어져 있으며, 뒤로 펼쳐진 먼 수평선과 구름의 형태는 자연스럽고 부드럽게 아웃포커싱되어 인물의 감정선에 몰입하게 만듭니다. 인물은 프레임 우측에 위치해 있고, 노을은 프레임 좌측 너머에 있습니다.

[배경 요소 (옵션)]

광활하고 잔잔한 바다 위로 화려하고 웅장한 노을이 펼쳐져 있습니다. 하늘에는 짙은 오렌지색과 결합된 거대한 보랏빛 뭉게구름들이 드라마틱하게 깔려 있으며, 물결이 거의 없는 바다는 이 하늘의 색채를 거울처럼 투명하게 반사하고 있습니다. 우측 먼 지평선 끝에는 희미하게 섬이나 해안가 건물의 실루엣이 낮게 깔려 있습니다.

[싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "CHAse"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다. 3:4비율
```

### 📷 例 1023：奇幻铁匠铺场景

![奇幻铁匠铺场景](../images/%E5%A5%87%E5%B9%BB%E9%93%81%E5%8C%A0%E9%93%BA%E5%9C%BA%E6%99%AF.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
创作一张黑暗奇幻风格的电影感插画，展示一位身处中世纪铁匠铺、肌肉发达的魁梧铁匠，采用腰部以上的竖构图。铁匠是一位留着乱糟糟长胡须的粗犷男子，{argument name="hair color" default="深棕色"} 的头发向后扎起，身穿厚重磨损的皮甲，戴着厚手套，配有皮带、金属铆钉和吊坠，但需在他面部正中央放置一个巨大的不透明深棕色遮挡方块。他左手紧握一把巨大的雕花铁锤，右手拿着火钳，正在沉重的黑色铁砧上锻打一块发着红橙色光的熔融金属刀刃或锭块。在撞击点，创造出橙色火花爆炸喷溅的效果，并混合着在炽热金属上蔓延的生动蓝紫色电弧，赋予场景一种摇滚风格的魔法锻造能量。背景是一个杂乱的石木结构铁匠铺，左侧设有 1 个燃烧的熔炉拱门，上方悬挂着铁链，墙上挂着工具和旗帜，右侧放着一箱煤炭。运用戏剧性的暖色火光、深邃的阴影、粗犷的质感、煤烟、漂浮在空中的余烬，呈现高细节、写实绘画风格、史诗奇幻概念艺术、强烈的对比度、体积光，并采用 2:3 的竖向海报构图。在右下角添加一个优雅的小签名和日期，内容为 {argument name="date text" default="2026 年 6 月 9 日"} 和 {argument name="signature text" default="Oyagi"}。除此之外，画面中不得出现其他可读文字。

[English]
Create a dark fantasy cinematic illustration of a massive muscular blacksmith in a medieval forge, shown from the waist up in a vertical composition. The blacksmith is a rugged bearded man with long messy {argument name="hair color" default="dark brown"} hair tied back, wearing heavy worn leather armor, thick gloves, straps, metal rivets, and a pendant, but place a large opaque dark brown square censor block directly over the center of his face. He grips a huge engraved hammer in his left hand and tongs in his right, striking a glowing red-orange molten metal blade or ingot on a heavy black anvil. At the impact point, create an explosive burst of orange sparks mixed with vivid blue-purple electric arcs crawling across the hot metal, giving the scene a rock-and-roll magical forge energy. The background is a cluttered stone-and-timber smithy with exactly 1 blazing furnace arch on the left, hanging chains above, tools and banners on the walls, and a crate of coal on the right. Use dramatic warm firelight, deep shadows, gritty textures, soot, embers floating through the air, high detail, painterly realism, epic fantasy concept art, strong contrast, volumetric glow, and a vertical 2:3 poster framing. Add a small elegant signature and date in the bottom-right corner reading {argument name="date text" default="June 9, 2026"} and {argument name="signature text" default="Oyagi"}. No other readable text.
```

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
创作一张竖版的高级定制奇幻人像，主角为 {argument name="character name" default="一位匿名女性"}，半身像，呈左侧四分之三侧面姿势，脸部中心被一个不透明的桃色矩形色块刻意遮挡以保持匿名。她拥有蓬松飘逸的 {argument name="hair color" default="草莓金与铜红色"} 头发，发丝如被风吹起般向上向外飞扬，与巨大的绘画感花卉和织物形态融为一体。在她的头部和身体周围布置 6 个醒目的花卉元素：头部左上方有 2 朵硕大的红色木槿花，右侧有 1 朵巨大的青蓝色花朵，脸颊附近有 1 朵粉色雏菊，腰部有 1 朵蓝色花朵，紧身胸衣附近有 1 朵淡色小花。她身着华丽的抹胸高级定制礼服，礼服上覆盖着红、白、蓝、橙色的花瓣、亮片、蕾丝和油漆飞溅纹理，领口处佩戴着多色珠宝项链。添加戏剧性的半透明丝带和鲜艳的深红、朱红、珊瑚色、橙色、青色、钴蓝色、洋红色和白色的油漆飞溅，在周围旋转，营造出爆炸性的花卉头饰和抽象背景。使用干净的白色摄影棚背景，明亮的高调照明，浅景深，将光泽感的时尚摄影写实风格与超现实植物高级定制相结合，呈现极具细节的纹理、动态感、饱和的色彩，以及 9:16 的竖版构图。保持脸部色块平整，不包含任何面部特征或文字，避免出现任何标志或水印。

[English]
Create a vertical high-fashion fantasy portrait of {argument name="character name" default="an anonymous woman"} from the waist up, posed in three-quarter profile facing left, with her face deliberately covered by a centered opaque peach rectangular block for anonymity. She has voluminous flowing {argument name="hair color" default="strawberry-blonde and copper-red"} hair whipped upward and outward as if caught in wind, merging with enormous painterly floral and fabric forms. Surround her head and body with exactly 6 prominent flower elements: 2 large red hibiscus-like blossoms above and left of the head, 1 large teal-blue flower on the right side, 1 pink daisy near the cheek area, 1 blue flower at the waist, and 1 small pale flower near the bodice. Dress her in an ornate strapless couture gown covered in red, white, blue, and orange petals, sequins, lace, and splattered paint textures, with a jeweled multicolor necklace at the neckline. Add dramatic translucent ribbons and paint-like splashes in vivid crimson, vermilion, coral, orange, teal, cobalt, magenta, and white swirling around her, creating an explosive floral headdress and abstract background. Use a clean white studio backdrop, bright high-key lighting, shallow depth of field, glossy editorial photography realism mixed with surreal botanical couture, extremely detailed textures, dynamic motion, saturated colors, and a vertical 9:16 composition. Keep the face block plain and flat with no facial features or text, avoid logos or watermarks.
```

### 📷 例 1030：巴西世界杯球星卡

![巴西世界杯球星卡](../images/%E5%B7%B4%E8%A5%BF%E4%B8%96%E7%95%8C%E6%9D%AF%E7%90%83%E6%98%9F%E5%8D%A1.jpg)

**Prompt:**

```text
[中文]
目标：为 {argument name="tournament title" default="World Cup 2026"} 创作一张精美的收藏级足球贴纸/卡片，画面包含巴西球迷/球员肖像，背景为极具张力的体育场。

画布：垂直 3:4 体育交易卡构图，采用半身特写镜头，配以受巴西配色启发的优质绿金金属边框。

主体：画面中心为一位自信的巴西女性足球迷/球员，留着长波浪金发，身穿带有绿色镶边的黄色巴西国家队球衣。球衣上需准确显示 4 处标记：观众视角左胸处有一个绿色 Nike 风格的勾形标志，右胸处有一个带星的巴西/CBF 队徽，队徽下方印有“BRASIL”字样，躯干中心印有一个醒目的绿色数字“10”。面部区域应覆盖一个纯色矩形肤色占位块，如同经过匿名处理或为用户肖像预留位置。

背景：使用拥挤的足球场作为背景，呈现模糊的黄绿色人群、明亮的体育场灯光、蓝天以及景深模糊效果。体育场背景左侧包含一面巨大的飘扬巴西国旗，右侧远处的记分牌上显示一个绿黄色调的奖杯图标。

顶部元素：在顶部中心放置一个带有金色边缘的深绿色斜面铭牌，内含白色斜体文字“{argument name="tournament title" default="World Cup 2026"}”。添加 2 个顶部角标：左上角为包含巴西国旗的圆角矩形徽章；右上角为包含黑白足球和三颗白星的盾形徽章。

底部元素：在左下角添加一个巨大的金色 FIFA 风格世界杯奖杯，与边框重叠。底部横跨一个宽大的黑色纹理金属铭牌，带有金色斜面和螺丝，铭牌内包含巨大的金色斜体名称“{argument name="player name" default="Danviane Aetherlux"}”。下方添加一个带有金色镶边的小型绿色牌匾，上面写着“{argument name="sticker code" default="FAN-01"}”。

风格：超写实体育海报与收藏级贴纸美学，清晰的光泽高光，压花金属金边，饱和的巴西绿/黄配色，电影级灯光，高对比度，精致的商业质感。

约束：保留以下可见文本：“World Cup 2026”、“Danviane Aetherlux”、“FAN-01”、“BRASIL” 和 “10”。使用 2 个顶部徽章和 1 个奖杯。请勿添加额外的徽标、水印、二维码或其他文字。

[English]
Goal: Create a glossy collectible soccer sticker/card for the {argument name="tournament title" default="World Cup 2026"}, featuring a Brazilian fan/player portrait in a dramatic stadium setting.

Canvas: Vertical 3:4 sports trading-card composition, tightly framed from mid-torso to head, with a premium green-and-gold metallic border inspired by Brazil colors.

[English]
[인물의 포즈 및 표정]

{argument name="장소" default="에펠탑"} 중앙 하단에서 카메라를 아래로 당당하게 내려다보는 로우 앵글 포즈입니다. 상체는 프레임 우측을 향해 45도 틀어져 있고, 고개를 돌려 카메라를 내려다 보고 있습니다.바람에 날리는 머리카락 사이로 세련되고 쿨한 표정을 짓고 있으며, 카메라를 아련하면서도 자신감 넘치는 눈빛으로 가만히 응시하고 있습니다.

[인물 의상]

몸에 부드럽게 밀착되는 정갈하고 심플한 {argument name="의상" default="화이트 반소매 라운드넥 티셔츠"}를 입고 있습니다.

[인물 헤어스타일 및 메이크업 디테일]

머리카락은 바람을 맞아 자연스럽게 볼륨감이 살아서 얼굴 주변으로 흩날리고 있습니다. 실버 금속 테에 은은한 보랏빛이 도는 반투명 렌즈의 스퀘어 선글라스를 착용했으며, 촉촉한 연분홍색 립글로스를 바른 내추럴하고 깨끗한 메이크업입니다.

[조명 및 빛 방향]

인물에 대한 직접 조명은 전혀 없으며 에펠탑 아래에서 은은하게 자연광이 있을 뿐입니다. 거대한 건축물 아래의 짙은 음영과 대비되어 인물의 세련된 실루엣을 더욱 선명하고 깨끗하게 부각해 줍니다.

[질감과 색감 무드]

티셔츠의 부드러운 면 질감 그리고 배경을 가득 채운 에펠탑 구조물의 거칠고 단단한 금속 질감이 선명한 대비를 이룹니다. 화이트와 블랙의 화사한 색감과 철골, 푸른 하늘이 조화를 이루어 시크하고 트렌디한 {argument name="도시" default="파리"} 여행 화보 무드를 자아냅니다.

[필름 및 카메라 렌즈 심도, 앵글]

바닥에서 하늘을 수직에 가깝게 올려다보는 극단적인 로우 앵글(웜즈 아이 뷰)로 촬영되었습니다. 인물의 허리 위부터 얼굴까지의 상반신을 화면 가득 담아낸 클로즈업 숏 구도이며, 선명한 스마트폰 카메라 렌즈를 사용하여 전면의 인물에게 초점을 맞추고 뒤편의 거대한 철골 구조물은 자연스럽게 아웃포커싱 처리했습니다.

[배경 요소 (옵션)]

화면 전체를 거대하게 감싸며 가로지르는 파리 에펠탑의 정교하고 거대한 짙은 회색 철골 격자 구조물입니다. 아치형 철골 기둥의 복잡하고 기하학적인 디테일이 인물 바로 뒤를 압도하듯 채우고 있으며, 촘촘한 철골 틈새 사이사이로 맑고 청명한 파란 하늘이 선명하게 들여다보입니다.

[싸인] 우측하단에는 Meta가 운영하는 Threads 로고가 작게 위치하고, 그 위에 흰색 필기체로 "CHAse"라고 싸인처럼 작게 적혀 있다. 4.5:비율

Use cinematic chiaroscuro lighting, warm brass highlights, cool gray shadows, shallow depth of field, ultra-detailed metal engraving, realistic fabric folds, symmetrical sacred composition, and a majestic yet slightly eerie mood. ### 📷 例 1094：动漫女仆角色情书
